```sql
               AND char_length(btrim(reject_reason)) >= 4));

-- 需要退费必须有退款金额与状态
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_change
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_chg_refund
    CHECK (refund_required = FALSE
           OR (refund_amount_snap IS NOT NULL
               AND refund_amount_snap >= 0
               AND refund_status_snap IS NOT NULL));

-- 扣违约金必须有金额
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_change
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_chg_penalty
    CHECK (penalty_flag = FALSE
           OR (penalty_amount_snap IS NOT NULL AND penalty_amount_snap >= 0));

-- 金额快照非负（金额真值由 M16 提供）
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_change
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_chg_amounts
    CHECK ((refund_amount_snap IS NULL OR refund_amount_snap >= 0)
           AND (penalty_amount_snap IS NULL OR penalty_amount_snap >= 0));

-- 已通知必须有通知时间与渠道
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_change
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_chg_notified
    CHECK (patient_notified = FALSE
           OR (notified_at IS NOT NULL AND notify_channel IS NOT NULL));

-- 通知失败必须有原因
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_change
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_chg_notify_fail
    CHECK (notify_failed_reason IS NULL OR patient_notified = FALSE);

-- 延期天数非负
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_change
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_chg_delay
    CHECK (delay_days IS NULL OR delay_days >= 0);

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_reservation_change IS '停台与改期表：记录每次取消/改期的决策链，支撑取消原因分析与改期链溯源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_change.change_seq IS '该预约的第几次变更，从 1 起递增';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_change.new_reservation_no IS '改期生成的新预约号，与原预约的 parent_reservation_no 互指';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_change.is_hospital_side IS '是否院方原因，用于责任划分（院方原因不扣违约金并计入质量统计）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_change.approval_required IS '手术前 24 小时内停台必须审批，避免手术间空转';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_change.refund_amount_snap IS '退款金额快照，由 M16 提供，M11 不计算任何金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_change.affect_quality_stat IS '是否计入质量统计，院内原因停台计入，患者原因单列';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么 `change_seq` 独立编号，不用主键顺序？**
   一次预约的完整历史可能是：改期（第 1 次）→ 改期（第 2 次）→ 取消（第 3 次）。用 `change_seq` 显式编号后，详情页时间轴可以直接按 `change_seq` 排序，不受插入时间精度影响；同时唯一索引 `(reservation_id, change_seq)` 天然防重复写入（网络重试导致的重复请求会被数据库拒绝）。

2. **为什么 `is_hospital_side` 与 `reason_category` 两个字段都要有？**
   `reason_category` 是客观归类（患者侧/医学/资源/外部），`is_hospital_side` 是**责任归属判定**（是否院方责任）。两者不总是一致：例如「医学原因停台」可能是患者术前感冒（患者侧），也可能是医师评估发现角膜偏薄不适合（院方医疗决策）。责任划分直接决定**是否扣违约金**与**是否计入院方质量指标**，必须分开存。

3. **为什么 `approval_required` 是落库字段而不是每次实时判断？**
   「手术前 24 小时内停台才需审批」这个规则会随时间变化——今天登记时距手术 3 天（不需审批），明天再看就变成 2 天了。若实时判断，一条历史记录的性质会随查询时间改变，统计口径就乱了。落库后固化「当时是否需审批」，与 `approval_no` 一起构成不可变的历史事实。

4. **为什么改期要有 `delay_days`（延期天数）字段？**
   延期天数直接影响患者体验与术中风险：延期 1~3 天通常仍在同意书有效期内（3 个月），若延期超过同意书有效期则必须重签（`new_consent_required`）；若延期超过 1 个月，部分患者术前检查结果（如角膜地形图、验血）需要复查。`delay_days` 是触发这些二次校验的输入，也是运营分析（平均延期天数）的直接数据源。

5. **为什么不物理删除变更记录？**
   停台与改期记录是**手术间利用率分析**与**医疗质量评价**的原始数据。若允许删除，「停台率高」的科室可以通过删记录来美化指标。只追加、只逻辑删除，且质量统计口径（`affect_quality_stat`）在写入时固化，保证统计不可事后篡改。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年新增 | 480 行 | 4,800 台 × 10% 停台改期率（含部分多次改期） |
| 10 年总量 | 4,800 行 | — |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 1,000 字节 | — |
| 表大小（10 年） | 约 5 MB | — |
| 索引大小（10 年） | 约 3 MB | 8 个索引 |

**分区：** 不分区。数据量极小，且需长期在线供运营回溯。

---

### 4.8 表：`m11_reservation_status_log`（预约状态流转日志表）

**用途：** 记录预约状态**每一次变化**的完整轨迹：从什么状态到什么状态、谁触发的、何时触发的、什么原因、附带什么数据。

**为什么状态流转要单独成表？**
主表只保留「当前状态」，但预约管理中最常被问的问题是：**「这台手术为什么没做成」、「它卡在哪个环节多久」**。例如：一台手术登记于 10-20，10-22 排台确认，10-25 进入准备，但 10-28 才开始准备（卡了 3 天），最终 10-31 完成。主表只能看到"已完成"，而流转日志能算出**每个环节的停留时长**，这是手术流程效率分析（周转时间、准备时长、超时原因）的唯一数据基础。

**写入方：** M11-05 全子模块（所有状态变更统一拦截写入）；**只读方：** P02 详情时间轴、P08 看板、M11-08 运营分析、质控复盘。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| reservation_id | BIGINT | N | — | 预约 ID |
| reservation_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 预约号（冗余） |
| patient_id | BIGINT | N | — | 患者 ID（冗余） |
| from_status | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 原状态（首次创建时为 NULL） |
| to_status | VARCHAR(16) | N | — | 目标状态 |
| transition_action | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 触发的动作：CREATE 创建 / SUBMIT 提交 / SCHEDULE 排台 / START_PREP 开始准备 / PREP_COMPLETE 准备完成 / START_SURGERY 开始手术 / COMPLETE 术毕 / CANCEL 取消 / POSTPONE 改期 / NO_SHOW 未到 |
| transition_source | VARCHAR(24) | N | 'USER' | 触发来源：USER 用户操作 / M06 M06 回调 / M09 M09 回调 / EVENT 事件订阅 / SYSTEM 定时任务 / API 内部接口 |
| dwell_seconds | INT | Y | NULL | 在原状态停留的秒数（服务层计算，用于流程效率分析） |
| dwell_level | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 停留时长分级：FAST 正常 / SLOW 偏慢 / STUCK 严重滞留（超阈值） |
| status_reason | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 状态变更原因 |
| change_snapshot | JSONB | Y | NULL | 本次变更的数据快照（变更的字段及前后值） |
| eye_side_snapshot | VARCHAR(8) | Y | NULL | 变更时的眼别快照（用于眼别一致性追溯） |
| related_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 关联单号（M06 申请号/台次号/M09 接收号/新预约号） |
| operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 操作人 ID（系统触发时为空） |
| operator_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 操作人姓名快照 |
| operator_role | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 操作角色：DOCTOR/NURSE/ADMIN/SYSTEM/API |
| operated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 状态变更时间（分区键） |
| trace_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 链路追踪 ID |
| org_id | BIGINT | N | 1 | 院区 ID |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间（与 operated_at 一致，分区键） |

**索引：**

```sql
-- 按预约查状态轨迹（详情页时间轴主力索引）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_stlog_reservation
    ON m11_reservation_status_log (reservation_id, operated_at);

-- 按日期 + 状态扫描（流程效率分析：某天停滞在哪个状态）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_stlog_status
    ON m11_reservation_status_log (to_status, operated_at);

-- 严重滞留记录（流程瓶颈分析）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_stlog_stuck
    ON m11_reservation_status_log (operated_at)
    WHERE dwell_level = 'STUCK';

-- 按操作人审计（质控与责任追溯）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_stlog_operator
    ON m11_reservation_status_log (operator_id, operated_at);

-- 按来源分析（M06/M09 回调的响应及时性）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_stlog_source
    ON m11_reservation_status_log (transition_source, operated_at);
```

**约束：**

```sql
-- 目标状态枚举（10 态）
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_to_status
    CHECK (to_status IN ('DRAFT', 'SUBMITTED', 'CONFIRMED', 'PREPARING', 'READY',
                         'IN_SURGERY', 'SURGERY_DONE', 'CANCELLED',
                         'POSTPONED', 'NO_SHOW'));

-- 原状态枚举（可为 NULL 表示创建）
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_from_status
    CHECK (from_status IS NULL
           OR from_status IN ('DRAFT', 'SUBMITTED', 'CONFIRMED', 'PREPARING', 'READY',
                              'IN_SURGERY', 'SURGERY_DONE', 'CANCELLED',
                              'POSTPONED', 'NO_SHOW'));

-- 动作枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_action
    CHECK (transition_action IN ('CREATE', 'SUBMIT', 'SCHEDULE', 'START_PREP',
                                 'PREP_COMPLETE', 'START_SURGERY', 'COMPLETE',
                                 'CANCEL', 'POSTPONE', 'NO_SHOW'));

-- 触发来源枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_source
    CHECK (transition_source IN ('USER', 'M06', 'M09', 'EVENT', 'SYSTEM', 'API'));

-- 停留分级枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_dwell
    CHECK (dwell_level IS NULL OR dwell_level IN ('FAST', 'SLOW', 'STUCK'));

-- 操作角色枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_role
    CHECK (operator_role IS NULL
           OR operator_role IN ('DOCTOR', 'NURSE', 'ADMIN', 'SYSTEM', 'API'));

-- 状态必须真的发生变化（防止无意义日志）
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_changed
    CHECK (from_status IS NULL OR from_status <> to_status);

-- 创建日志必须无原状态，非创建必须有原状态
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_create
    CHECK ((transition_action = 'CREATE' AND from_status IS NULL)
           OR (transition_action <> 'CREATE' AND from_status IS NOT NULL));

-- 停留时长非负
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_dwell_sec
    CHECK (dwell_seconds IS NULL OR dwell_seconds >= 0);

-- 眼别快照合法
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_status_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_stlog_eye
    CHECK (eye_side_snapshot IS NULL
           OR eye_side_snapshot IN ('OD', 'OS', 'OU'));

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_reservation_status_log IS '预约状态流转日志：记录每次状态变化的完整轨迹与停留时长，是流程效率分析的基础';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_status_log.dwell_seconds IS '在原状态停留的秒数，由服务层计算，用于识别流程瓶颈';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_status_log.dwell_level IS 'FAST 正常 / SLOW 偏慢 / STUCK 严重滞留（超系统参数阈值）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_status_log.eye_side_snapshot IS '变更时的眼别快照，用于追溯眼别是否在流转中被意外修改';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_status_log.transition_source IS 'M06/M09 回演与 EVENT 事件订阅的区分，用于分析跨模块响应及时性';
```

**分区策略：**

```sql
-- 按月 RANGE 分区，保留 36 个月（质量复盘与流程分析窗口）
CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_status_log (
    id              BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
    reservation_id  BIGINT NOT NULL,
    reservation_no  VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    to_status       VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
    operated_at     TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_at      TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT pk_m11_reservation_status_log PRIMARY KEY (id, operated_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (operated_at);

-- 2026 年 11 月分区示例（其余月份由 m11_resv_stlog_partition 任务每月 1 日预建）
CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_status_log_p202611
    PARTITION OF m11_reservation_status_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01');

CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_status_log_p202612
    PARTITION OF m11_reservation_status_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-12-01') TO ('2027-01-01');

-- DEFAULT 分区兜底（防止未预建月份写入失败）
CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_status_log_pdefault
    PARTITION OF m11_reservation_status_log DEFAULT;
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么 `dwell_seconds` 与 `dwell_level` 由服务层计算并落库，而不是查询时算？**
   流程效率分析需要按「状态 × 停留时长」聚合（例如「PREPARING 状态平均停留 48 小时，超过 24 小时的占 30%」）。若查询时算，就要对每行做 `LAG()` 窗口函数取上一条记录相减，10 万行级别尚可，但**历史数据一旦归档到冷存，窗口函数就无法跨表计算**。落库后无论数据在哪里，聚合都只需 `AVG(dwell_seconds)` 一次扫描。

2. **为什么状态日志要按月分区？**
   这是本子模块唯一的高频写入表（日均 92 条，年 3.36 万条，10 年 33.6 万条）。虽然总量不大，但特点是**只追加、按时间窗口查询**（分析通常只看近 12 个月）。按月分区后：①近月查询只扫 1~2 个分区；②超过 36 个月的分区可整块 `DETACH` 归档到冷存（这 33 万行整体很小，但分区让归档变成 O(1) 操作而非 DELETE）；③`DEFAULT` 分区兜底保证任何遗漏建分区的月份都不会写入失败。

3. **为什么分区表主键必须是 `(id, operated_at)`？**
   PostgreSQL 分区表硬性要求主键包含全部分区键列，否则无法保证全局唯一。这不是设计选择，是引擎约束，因此主键声明为复合键。

4. **为什么 `eye_side_snapshot` 要在状态日志里再存一次？**
   眼别是手术安全的核心字段，若在某个状态流转中被意外修改（例如 M06 回调把眼别从 `OD` 改成了 `OS`），主表只有最终值无法发现。在每次状态变更时快照眼别，就能通过时间轴对比找出「眼别在哪一步被改了、谁改的」。这是眼别一致性的**纵向审计维度**，与 P06 核对表的横向审计（四个来源比对）互补。

5. **为什么 `ck_m11_resv_stlog_changed` 禁止 `from_status = to_status`？**
   无变化的状态日志是噪音，会污染停留时长统计（把一次刷新算成一次流转）。数据库层面拒绝，强制服务层只在真实变化时写日志。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年新增 | 33,600 行 | 4,800 台 × 7 次流转 |
| 月分区大小 | 约 2,800 行 / 约 3 MB | 含索引约 5 MB |
| 保留 36 个月 | 100,800 行 / 约 108 MB | 含索引约 180 MB |
| 单分区查询 | 索引扫描 < 3ms | 详情页时间轴按 `reservation_id` 命中 |

---

### 4.9 表：`m11_reservation_daily`（手术日看板日汇总表）

**用途：** 按「手术日期 + 主刀医师 + 手术间」预聚合手术量与流程指标，为 P08 手术日看板与 M11-08 运营分析提供 O(1) 查询。

**为什么不直接实时聚合 `m11_reservation`？**
P08 看板需要展示「今日各手术间、各术者的手术量、完成率、准时率、平均周转」。若每次都实时聚合，一次看板刷新要扫当日全部预约 + 关联用药/核对/流转表做多重 JOIN，日均 25 台的日期尚可，但看板还有「本月趋势」「近 7 日对比」等视图，实时聚合会随数据增长不断变慢。预聚合后看板查询退化为一次主键/唯一键查找，稳定在 5ms 内。

**写入方：** M11-05 定时任务（每小时刷新当日 + 夜间全量重算）；**只读方：** P08 看板、M11-08 运营分析。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| summary_date | DATE | N | — | 统计日期（手术日期） |
| surgeon_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 主刀医师 ID（NULL 表示该日全部医师汇总行） |
| surgeon_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 主刀医师姓名快照 |
| room_code | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 手术间编码（NULL 表示该日全部手术间汇总行） |
| facility_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 手术间名称快照 |
| dim_level | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'DETAIL' | 汇总维度：DETAIL 医师×手术间 / SURGEON 医师汇总 / ROOM 手术间汇总 / TOTAL 全院汇总 |
| planned_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 计划台次（当日该维度预约总数） |
| completed_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 完成台次（`SURGERY_DONE`） |
| cancelled_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 取消台次（`CANCELLED`） |
| postponed_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 改期台次（`POSTPONED`） |
| no_show_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 未到台次（`NO_SHOW`） |
| in_progress_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 术中（截至统计时刻 `IN_SURGERY`） |
| pending_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 待完成（`SUBMITTED`/`CONFIRMED`/`PREPARING`/`READY`） |
| completion_rate | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 完成率（%，`completed_count / planned_count`，服务层计算） |
| cancel_rate | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 取消率（%） |
| no_show_rate | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 爽约率（%） |
| hospital_cancel_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 院方原因取消台次（质量指标） |
| eye_side_od_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 右眼台次 |
| eye_side_os_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 左眼台次 |
| eye_side_ou_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 双眼同台台次 |
| type_smile_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | SMILE 台次 |
| type_fs_lasik_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | FS-LASIK 台次 |
| type_transprk_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | TransPRK 台次 |
| type_icl_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | ICL 台次 |
| type_other_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 其他术式台次 |
| ready_on_time_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 准时可开台台次（`ready_at` 早于计划开台时间） |
| on_time_rate | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 准时开台率（%，服务层计算） |
| avg_prep_minutes | NUMERIC(8,2) | Y | NULL | 平均术前准备时长（分钟，`confirmed_at` → `ready_at`，服务层计算） |
| avg_turnover_minutes | NUMERIC(8,2) | Y | NULL | 平均周转时长（分钟，上一台 `actual_end_time` → 本台 `actual_start_time`，服务层计算） |
| avg_surgery_minutes | NUMERIC(8,2) | Y | NULL | 平均手术时长（分钟，`actual_start_time` → `actual_end_time`，服务层计算） |
| max_delay_minutes | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 当日最大延迟分钟数（实际开台 − 计划开台，取最大值） |
| checklist_blocked_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 核对硬阻断次数（安全质量指标） |
| eye_mismatch_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | **眼别不一致发现次数（核心安全指标，发现即为拦截成功）** |
| consent_missing_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 同意书缺失台次（术前被拦截） |
| med_incomplete_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 术前用药未完成台次 |
| refresh_mode | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'HOURLY' | 刷新方式：HOURLY 每小时增量 / NIGHTLY 夜间全量 / MANUAL 手工重算 |
| last_refreshed_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 最近刷新时间 |
| org_id | BIGINT | N | 1 | 院区 ID |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引：**

```sql
-- 同一天、同一维度、同一医师、同一手术间只能有一条汇总
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_resv_daily_dim
    ON m11_reservation_daily (summary_date, dim_level,
                              COALESCE(surgeon_id, 0), COALESCE(room_code, ''))
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按日期取看板（P08 主力索引）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_daily_date
    ON m11_reservation_daily (summary_date DESC, dim_level)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 按医师查历史（医师业绩与效率分析）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_daily_surgeon
    ON m11_reservation_daily (surgeon_id, summary_date DESC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND surgeon_id IS NOT NULL;

-- 按手术间查利用率
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_daily_room
    ON m11_reservation_daily (room_code, summary_date DESC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE AND room_code IS NOT NULL;

-- 安全指标异常日（眼别不一致或阻断次数 > 0 的日子，质控重点关注）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_daily_safety
    ON m11_reservation_daily (summary_date DESC)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
      AND (eye_mismatch_count > 0 OR checklist_blocked_count > 0);
```

**约束：**

```sql
-- 汇总维度枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_dim
    CHECK (dim_level IN ('DETAIL', 'SURGEON', 'ROOM', 'TOTAL'));

-- 刷新方式枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_refresh
    CHECK (refresh_mode IN ('HOURLY', 'NIGHTLY', 'MANUAL'));

-- 台次数均非负
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_counts
    CHECK (planned_count >= 0 AND completed_count >= 0 AND cancelled_count >= 0
           AND postponed_count >= 0 AND no_show_count >= 0
           AND in_progress_count >= 0 AND pending_count >= 0
           AND hospital_cancel_count >= 0
           AND eye_side_od_count >= 0 AND eye_side_os_count >= 0
           AND eye_side_ou_count >= 0
           AND type_smile_count >= 0 AND type_fs_lasik_count >= 0
           AND type_transprk_count >= 0 AND type_icl_count >= 0
           AND type_other_count >= 0
           AND ready_on_time_count >= 0
           AND checklist_blocked_count >= 0 AND eye_mismatch_count >= 0
           AND consent_missing_count >= 0 AND med_incomplete_count >= 0);

-- 完成 + 取消 + 改期 + 未到 + 术中 + 待完成 不得超过计划台次
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_sum
    CHECK (completed_count + cancelled_count + postponed_count + no_show_count
           + in_progress_count + pending_count <= planned_count);

-- 完成数不得超过计划数
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_completed
    CHECK (completed_count <= planned_count);

-- 眼别分布之和不得超过完成台次
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_eye_sum
    CHECK (eye_side_od_count + eye_side_os_count + eye_side_ou_count
           <= planned_count);

-- 术式分布之和不得超过计划台次
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_type_sum
    CHECK (type_smile_count + type_fs_lasik_count + type_transprk_count
           + type_icl_count + type_other_count <= planned_count);

-- 准时台次不得超过完成台次
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_ontime
    CHECK (ready_on_time_count <= completed_count + in_progress_count);

-- 院方取消不得超过取消总数
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_hospital_cancel
    CHECK (hospital_cancel_count <= cancelled_count);

-- 比率范围 0~100
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_rates
    CHECK ((completion_rate IS NULL OR (completion_rate >= 0 AND completion_rate <= 100))
           AND (cancel_rate IS NULL OR (cancel_rate >= 0 AND cancel_rate <= 100))
           AND (no_show_rate IS NULL OR (no_show_rate >= 0 AND no_show_rate <= 100))
           AND (on_time_rate IS NULL OR (on_time_rate >= 0 AND on_time_rate <= 100)));

-- 时长指标非负
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_durations
    CHECK ((avg_prep_minutes IS NULL OR avg_prep_minutes >= 0)
           AND (avg_turnover_minutes IS NULL OR avg_turnover_minutes >= 0)
           AND (avg_surgery_minutes IS NULL OR avg_surgery_minutes >= 0)
           AND (max_delay_minutes IS NULL OR max_delay_minutes >= 0));

-- 医师/手术间汇总行必须有对应维度字段
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_daily
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_daily_detail
    CHECK ((dim_level <> 'DETAIL' OR (surgeon_id IS NOT NULL AND room_code IS NOT NULL))
           AND (dim_level <> 'SURGEON' OR (surgeon_id IS NOT NULL AND room_code IS NULL))
           AND (dim_level <> 'ROOM' OR (surgeon_id IS NULL AND room_code IS NOT NULL))
           AND (dim_level <> 'TOTAL' OR (surgeon_id IS NULL AND room_code IS NULL)));

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_reservation_daily IS '手术日看板日汇总：按日期×医师×手术间预聚合，支撑 P08 看板 O(1) 查询';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_daily.dim_level IS 'DETAIL 医师×手术间 / SURGEON 医师汇总 / ROOM 手术间汇总 / TOTAL 全院汇总';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_daily.eye_mismatch_count IS '眼别不一致发现次数；发现即被拦截即为成功，此值为安全监控核心指标';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_daily.completion_rate IS '完成率百分比，由服务层计算写入，M11 不涉及金额计算';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_daily.avg_turnover_minutes IS '平均周转时长（上一台下台到本台开台），手术间效率核心指标';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么用 `dim_level` 四层维度而不是只存物理明细行？**
   看板需要同时展示「按医师」「按手术间」「全院合计」三种视角。若只存 DETAIL 行（医师×手术间），查「医师汇总」需要先 `GROUP BY surgeon_id` 再聚合——而 DETAIL 行的粒度已经是医师×手术间，聚合是可行的，但会有「同一医师多个手术间」的重复计数问题。显式存 SURGEON / ROOM / TOTAL 三层汇总行后，每种视角都是**直接命中的唯一索引查找**，且汇总口径由写入任务统一保证一致（不会出现两个页面算出不同数字）。

2. **为什么唯一索引用 `COALESCE(surgeon_id, 0), COALESCE(room_code, '')`？**
   PostgreSQL 唯一索引中 NULL 互不相等（`NULL <> NULL`），若直接对可空列建唯一索引，TOTAL 行（两列都 NULL）可以被无限重复插入。用 `COALESCE` 把 NULL 归一化为哨兵值后，唯一性才真正生效。

3. **为什么安全指标（`eye_mismatch_count` / `checklist_blocked_count`）要进日汇总？**
   这两个指标是**质控的每日必看项**：眼别不一致发现次数应该 > 0（说明核对机制在起作用）但不能持续上升（说明前端环节质量差）；核对阻断次数突增意味着某环节系统性出问题。把安全指标纳入日汇总并建部分索引 `idx_m11_resv_daily_safety`，质控人员每天只需查「有异常的日子」，不必全表扫。

4. **为什么 `ck_m11_resv_daily_sum` 用 `<=` 而不是 `=`？**
   统计时刻可能有预约处于「流转中」（如刚 `CONFIRMED` 但尚未进入 `PREPARING` 的短暂状态），分类归入 `pending_count` 的边界可能因刷新时机而差 1~2 台。用 `<=` 允许这种瞬时差异，避免因统计口径边界导致的写入失败；夜间全量重算时数据收敛后差异自然消失。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年新增 | 3,120 行 | 365 天 × (DETAIL 约 6 + SURGEON 2 + ROOM 3 + TOTAL 1 ≈ 8.5) |
| 10 年总量 | 31,200 行 | — |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 420 字节 | — |
| 表大小（10 年） | 约 13 MB | — |
| 索引大小（10 年） | 约 11 MB | 5 个索引 |

**分区：** 不分区。数据量极小（10 年 3 万行），且需跨年趋势对比。

---

### 4.10 表：`m11_reservation_log`（操作审计日志表）

**用途：** 记录本子模块**所有写操作的审计轨迹**，包括挂号预约的创建、修改、状态变更、同意书签署、核对执行、停台改期、以及**所有查看敏感信息的行为**。

**为什么单独一张审计表，不复用 `m11_reservation_status_log`？**
两者职责完全不同：
- `m11_reservation_status_log` 记录**业务状态的变化**（预约本身走到了哪一步），是业务数据；
- `m11_reservation_log` 记录**人的操作行为**（谁在什么时候做了什么、看了什么），是审计数据。

审计日志必须覆盖**只读行为**（谁查看了患者身份证号、谁导出了同意书），而状态日志天然无法记录这类行为。此外审计日志有**防篡改要求**（只追加、应用账号无 UPDATE/DELETE 权限），与状态日志的保留策略也不同。

**写入方：** M11-05 服务层统一拦截（AOP 切面）；**只读方：** 审计部门、质控、信息科、法务。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| log_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 日志流水号 `LOG` + YYYYMMDD + 8 位流水，如 `LOG20261031000123` |
| trace_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 链路追踪 ID（与网关 trace 一致） |
| reservation_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 预约 ID（预约不存在时可为空，如批量导出） |
| reservation_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 预约号 |
| patient_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 患者 ID |
| patient_name_mask | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 患者姓名掩码（如「张*」，日志不落明文姓名） |
| action | VARCHAR(48) | N | — | 操作类型，见下方枚举 |
| action_group | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'BUSINESS' | 操作分组：BUSINESS 业务 / SAFETY 安全 / CONSENT 同意书 / AUDIT 审计浏览 / EXPORT 导出 / CONFIG 配置 |
| api_path | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 调用的接口路径 |
| http_method | VARCHAR(8) | Y | NULL | HTTP 方法 GET/POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE |
| changed_fields | VARCHAR(1000) | Y | NULL | 变更字段列表（逗号分隔） |
| before_snapshot | JSONB | Y | NULL | 变更前快照（**敏感字段已脱敏**） |
| after_snapshot | JSONB | Y | NULL | 变更后快照（**敏感字段已脱敏**） |
| change_summary | VARCHAR(1024) | Y | NULL | 变更摘要（人可读） |
| change_reason | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 变更原因（关键操作必填） |
| is_sensitive | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否涉及敏感信息（证件号、联系方式、同意书正文） |
| is_safety_related | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | **是否与手术安全相关（眼别、核对、同意书）** |
| approval_no | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 关联审批单号 |
| operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 操作人职工 ID |
| operator_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 操作人姓名快照 |
| operator_role | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 操作人角色 |
| operator_dept | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 操作人科室 |
| operator_ip | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 操作 IP |
| client_type | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 客户端类型：WEB/PAD/MOBILE/API/BATCH |
| result | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'SUCCESS' | 操作结果：SUCCESS / FAILED / REJECTED |
| error_code | VARCHAR(8) | Y | NULL | 失败时的错误码（如 `"1105012"`） |
| error_msg | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 失败原因 |
| affected_rows | INT | Y | NULL | 影响行数（批量操作时） |
| operated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 操作时间（分区键） |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间（与 operated_at 一致，分区键） |

**索引：**

```sql
-- 日志流水号唯一（分区表唯一索引需含分区键）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_resv_log_no
    ON m11_reservation_log (log_no, operated_at);

-- 按预约查操作轨迹
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_reservation
    ON m11_reservation_log (reservation_id, operated_at DESC);

-- 按操作人审计（合规调查）
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_operator
    ON m11_reservation_log (operator_id, operated_at DESC);

-- 按操作类型统计
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_action
    ON m11_reservation_log (action, operated_at DESC);

-- 【安全审计】安全相关操作专项索引
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_safety
    ON m11_reservation_log (operated_at DESC)
    WHERE is_safety_related = TRUE;

-- 【合规审计】敏感信息访问清单
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_sensitive
    ON m11_reservation_log (operated_at DESC)
    WHERE is_sensitive = TRUE;

-- 失败操作排查
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_failed
    ON m11_reservation_log (result, operated_at DESC)
    WHERE result <> 'SUCCESS';

-- 链路追踪
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_resv_log_trace
    ON m11_reservation_log (trace_id)
    WHERE trace_id IS NOT NULL;
```

**约束：**

```sql
-- 日志流水号格式：LOG + 8 位日期 + 8 位流水
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_no_format
    CHECK (log_no ~ '^LOG[0-9]{16}$');

-- 操作类型枚举（覆盖本子模块全部写与敏感读操作）
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_action
    CHECK (action IN (
        'CREATE', 'UPDATE', 'SUBMIT', 'SCHEDULE_CONFIRM',
        'START_PREP', 'PREP_COMPLETE', 'START_SURGERY', 'COMPLETE',
        'CANCEL', 'POSTPONE', 'NO_SHOW', 'REASSIGN', 'CHANGE_ROOM',
        'EYE_SIDE_MARK', 'EYE_SIDE_CHANGE', 'EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH_BLOCK',
        'CHECKLIST_EXEC', 'CHECKLIST_PASS', 'CHECKLIST_FAIL', 'CHECKLIST_BLOCK',
        'CHECKLIST_UNBLOCK',
        'CONSENT_GENERATE', 'CONSENT_SIGN', 'CONSENT_REVOKE', 'CONSENT_EXPIRE',
        'CONSENT_TAMPER_CHECK',
        'MED_EXEC', 'MED_SKIP', 'MED_ADVERSE',
        'EDU_EXEC', 'EDU_CONFIRM',
        'M06_PUSH', 'M06_CANCEL_PUSH', 'P09_CALLBACK',
        'VIEW_SENSITIVE', 'VIEW_CONSENT', 'EXPORT', 'PRINT',
        'CONFIG_UPDATE'));

-- 操作分组枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_group
    CHECK (action_group IN ('BUSINESS', 'SAFETY', 'CONSENT', 'AUDIT',
                            'EXPORT', 'CONFIG'));

-- 操作结果枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_result
    CHECK (result IN ('SUCCESS', 'FAILED', 'REJECTED'));

-- 客户端类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_client
    CHECK (client_type IS NULL
           OR client_type IN ('WEB', 'PAD', 'MOBILE', 'API', 'BATCH'));

-- HTTP 方法枚举
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_method
    CHECK (http_method IS NULL
           OR http_method IN ('GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'));

-- 失败必须记录错误码与原因
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_failed
    CHECK (result = 'SUCCESS'
           OR (error_code IS NOT NULL AND error_msg IS NOT NULL));

-- 敏感操作必须有原因
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_sensitive
    CHECK (is_sensitive = FALSE
           OR (change_reason IS NOT NULL
               AND char_length(btrim(change_reason)) >= 4));

-- 眼别变更必须标记为安全相关且为敏感，并必须有审批单号
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_eye_change
    CHECK (action <> 'EYE_SIDE_CHANGE'
           OR (is_safety_related = TRUE
               AND is_sensitive = TRUE
               AND approval_no IS NOT NULL
               AND changed_fields IS NOT NULL));

-- 眼别不一致拦截必须标记为安全相关
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_eye_block
    CHECK (action NOT IN ('EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH_BLOCK', 'CHECKLIST_BLOCK')
           OR is_safety_related = TRUE);

-- 更新类操作必须有变更字段与摘要
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_changed
    CHECK (action NOT IN ('UPDATE', 'EYE_SIDE_CHANGE', 'REASSIGN', 'CHANGE_ROOM')
           OR (changed_fields IS NOT NULL AND change_summary IS NOT NULL));

-- 业务操作必须有预约标识（浏览类与导出类除外）
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_target
    CHECK (action IN ('VIEW_SENSITIVE', 'VIEW_CONSENT', 'EXPORT', 'PRINT',
                      'CONFIG_UPDATE', 'SEARCH')
           OR reservation_no IS NOT NULL);

-- 影响行数非负
ALTER TABLE m11_reservation_log
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_resv_log_affected
    CHECK (affected_rows IS NULL OR affected_rows >= 0);

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_reservation_log IS '操作审计日志：只追加，禁止 UPDATE 业务内容与物理 DELETE，覆盖写操作与敏感读操作，按月分区保留 36 个月';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_log.patient_name_mask IS '患者姓名掩码，日志不落明文姓名（隐私最小化）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_log.is_safety_related IS '是否与手术安全相关（眼别、核对、同意书），质控与安全审计重点';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_log.is_sensitive IS '是否涉及敏感信息，为 TRUE 时必须有变更原因';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_reservation_log.operated_at IS '操作时间，同时是分区键';
```

**分区策略：**

```sql
-- 按月 RANGE 分区，保留 36 个月（合规审计 3 年）
CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_log (
    id           BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
    log_no       VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    action       VARCHAR(48) NOT NULL,
    is_safety_related BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    operated_at  TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_at   TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT pk_m11_reservation_log PRIMARY KEY (id, operated_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (operated_at);

-- 月份分区示例（由 m11_resv_log_partition 任务每月 1 日 01:00 预建未来 3 个月）
CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_log_p202611
    PARTITION OF m11_reservation_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01');

CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_log_p202612
    PARTITION OF m11_reservation_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-12-01') TO ('2027-01-01');

-- DEFAULT 分区兜底
CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_log_pdefault
    PARTITION OF m11_reservation_log DEFAULT;
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么审计日志要覆盖只读操作（`VIEW_SENSITIVE` / `VIEW_CONSENT`）？**
   患者身份证号、联系方式、同意书正文属于敏感个人信息。合规审查最关心的不是「谁改了数据」，而是「**谁看了不该看的数据**」。某位护士批量查看 200 个患者的同意书，若系统无记录，就无法判断是正常工作还是信息泄露。记录敏感读取，才能做到「可控、可查、可追溯」。

2. **为什么 `is_safety_related` 要作为独立标记列并有专门索引？**
   安全相关操作（眼别标记、核对执行、眼别不一致拦截）在审计中的优先级远高于普通业务操作（如修改备注）。质控部门每月要调阅「所有眼别不一致拦截记录」做复盘，若只能按 `action IN (...)` 列表过滤，索引选择性与可维护性都差。独立布尔列 + 部分索引后，这条查询变成一次高选择性索引扫描。

3. **为什么 `EYE_SIDE_CHANGE` 强制要求 `approval_no`？**
   预约创建后修改眼别是**极高风险操作**——正常流程中眼别来源于手术方案，不应随意改动。若确需改动（如方案换版、患者临时改变主意只做单眼），必须走审批：科主任确认「确实要改」，并留下审批单号。这条约束把「偷偷改眼别」的可能性在数据库层封死。

4. **为什么 `patient_name_mask` 存掩码而 `before_snapshot` 也要脱敏？**
   审计日志的读取者（审计、质控）范围比业务表更宽。若日志存明文，等于把患者隐私泄露给了本不该看到的人。姓名掩码 + 快照脱敏 + 保留 `patient_id` 的组合，使审计人员既能定位记录（通过 ID 和预约号），又能做到隐私最小化（看不到完整姓名、证件、电话）。确需查看明文时，通过业务接口并再次留痕。

5. **为什么不物理删除、不 UPDATE？**
   审计日志的法律价值建立在**不可篡改性**上。应用数据库账号对该表只有 `INSERT` 与 `SELECT` 权限（无 `UPDATE`/`DELETE`），超 36 个月的分区通过 `DETACH` + 归档到对象存储后 `DROP`，全过程不经过 `DELETE` 语句。这样即便应用被攻破，攻击者也改不了历史审计记录。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年新增 | 57,600 行 | 4,800 台 × 12 条（含敏感浏览） |
| 月分区大小 | 约 4,800 行 / 约 6 MB | 含索引约 10 MB |
| 保留 36 个月 | 172,800 行 / 约 220 MB | 含索引约 360 MB |
| 单分区查询 | 索引扫描 < 5ms | 「按预约查操作轨迹」只命中近月分区 |

**分区理由：** 本表是 M11-05 写入最频繁的表（日均 158 条），且访问模式是「时间窗口内的合规核查」（「查一下 10 月所有眼别变更记录」），不查跨年全量。按月分区后老分区整块归档，在线表只保留 36 个月，表体积与查询性能都不随时间劣化。

---

### 4.11 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义

```python
# app/models/m11/reservation.py
"""M11-05 手术预约与术前准备 —— SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity, Index,
    Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, Text, func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.models.base import Base


class M11Reservation(Base):
    """手术预约主表。

    核心约定：
    1) eye_side 是眼别安全校验的权威来源，只允许 OD/OS/OU，禁 NA；
    2) 金额字段一律带 _snap 后缀，值为 M16 返回的快照，M11 不计算；
    3) 术中数据（actual_start_time / intraop_summary_snap）由 M09 事件回填，
       M11 不写术中记录。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=False, doc="手术预约号 YY+YYYYMMDD+4位")

    # ---------- 患者信息组（M03 只读引用 + 快照） ----------
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, doc="患者ID，只读引用 M03 mpi_patient.id")
    empi_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    patient_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    patient_gender: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    patient_age: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    patient_phone: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    id_card_mask: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 上游单据引用组（只读，不建外键） ----------
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    plan_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=False, doc="手术方案号，只读引用 M11-04")
    plan_version: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    admission_result_snap: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    optometry_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 手术方案组 ----------
    refractive_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, doc="屈光术式，与 M01-07 3.1.4 一字不差")
    operation_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    operation_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 【核心安全组】眼别 ----------
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False,
        doc="手术眼别 OD/OS/OU，禁 NA；错眼是屈光手术最严重事故")
    eye_side_plan_snap: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=True, doc="方案眼别快照，与 eye_side 不一致则数据库拒绝")
    laterality_mark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=True, doc="术眼标记（患者身体上的物理标记）")
    laterality_marked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    laterality_marked_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    laterality_witness_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True, doc="术眼标记见证人，不能与执行人相同")

    # ---------- 排程组 ----------
    surgery_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    surgery_weekday: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    time_slot: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    planned_start_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    planned_end_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    sequence_no: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    anest_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    anest_config_snap: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 人员组 ----------
    surgeon_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    surgeon_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    surgeon_auth_snap: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    assistant_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    assistant_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    anesthesiologist_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    nurse_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    dept_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    dept_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    store_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 状态组 ----------
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="DRAFT", server_default="DRAFT")
    surgery_status_snap: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=True, doc="M06/M09 执行态快照，M11 只读")
    surgery_status_snap_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---------- M06/M09 集成组 ----------
    m06_apply_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    m06_apply_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    m06_schedule_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    m09_reception_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    m06_sync_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="NOT_PUSHED",
        server_default="NOT_PUSHED")
    m06_push_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    m06_push_error: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 术前准备完成度组 ----------
    consent_completed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    consent_completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    preop_med_completed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    preop_med_completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    education_completed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    education_completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    checkin_completed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    checkin_completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    fasting_confirmed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    preop_checklist_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 金额快照组（M16 提供，M11 不计算） ----------
    payment_status_snap: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    plan_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 4), nullable=True, doc="方案金额快照，M16 提供")
    deposit_paid_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 4), nullable=True)
    total_paid_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 4), nullable=True)
    billing_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 来源组 ----------
    priority_flag: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="NORMAL", server_default="NORMAL")
    reservation_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="HOSPITAL", server_default="HOSPITAL")
    consult_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    parent_reservation_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    change_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    facility_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    facility_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    room_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 术中快照组（M09 事件回填，M11 不写术中记录） ----------
    actual_start_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True, doc="实际开台，M09 事件回填")
    actual_end_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    surgery_completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    surgery_outcome_snap: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    intraop_summary_snap: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True, doc="术中摘要快照，来自 m09.surgery.completed 事件")

    # ---------- 随访组 ----------
    followup_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    followup_generated: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    # ---------- 终止组 ----------
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    cancel_reason_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    cancelled_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    cancelled_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    postpone_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    no_show_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 时间戳组 ----------
    submitted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    confirmed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    ready_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 标准字段 ----------
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024), nullable=True)
    ext_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_reservation_no", "reservation_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_reservation_patient_date_eye",
              "patient_id", "surgery_date", "eye_side", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND status NOT IN "
                  "('CANCELLED', 'POSTPONED', 'NO_SHOW')")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_date", "surgery_date", "room_code", "sequence_no",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_patient", "patient_id",
              text("surgery_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_plan", "plan_no",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_surgeon", "surgeon_id", "surgery_date",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_active", "surgery_date", "status",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND status IN "
                  "('SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','PREPARING','READY','IN_SURGERY')")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_pending", "surgery_date",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND status IN ('CONFIRMED','PREPARING') "
                  "AND (consent_completed = FALSE OR preop_med_completed = FALSE "
                  "OR education_completed = FALSE OR checkin_completed = FALSE)")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_push_retry", "m06_sync_status", "m06_push_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND m06_sync_status IN ('FAILED','RETRYING')")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_followup_pending", "surgery_completed_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND status = 'SURGERY_DONE' "
                  "AND followup_generated = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_name_trgm", "patient_name",
              postgresql_using="gin",
              postgresql_ops={"patient_name": "gin_trgm_ops"}),
        Index("idx_m11_reservation_org", "org_id", "store_code",
              text("surgery_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint(r"reservation_no ~ '^YY[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU')", name="ck_m11_resv_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "eye_side_plan_snap IS NULL OR eye_side = eye_side_plan_snap",
            name="ck_m11_resv_eye_matches_plan"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "laterality_mark IS NULL OR laterality_mark IN ('OD','OS','OU') "
            "AND (eye_side = 'OU' OR laterality_mark = eye_side)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_laterality_match"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "laterality_mark IS NULL OR (laterality_marked_at IS NOT NULL "
            "AND laterality_marked_by IS NOT NULL "
            "AND laterality_witness_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND laterality_witness_id <> laterality_marked_by)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_laterality_complete"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','PREPARING','READY',"
            "'IN_SURGERY','SURGERY_DONE','CANCELLED','POSTPONED','NO_SHOW')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refractive_type IN ('SMILE','FS_LASIK','TRANSPRK','SMART','ICL',"
            "'PRL','CXL','PIOL','RK','OTHER_REFRACTIVE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_refractive_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "anest_type IS NULL OR anest_type IN "
            "('SURFACE','LOCAL','SEDATION','GENERAL')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_anest_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "time_slot IS NULL OR time_slot IN ('AM','PM','EVENING')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_time_slot"),
        CheckConstraint("priority_flag IN ('NORMAL','URGENT','VIP')",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_priority"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "reservation_source IN ('HOSPITAL','ONLINE','PHONE','REFERRAL','RESCHEDULE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_source"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "m06_sync_status IN ('NOT_PUSHED','PUSHED','FAILED','RETRYING')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_sync_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "payment_status_snap IS NULL OR payment_status_snap IN "
            "('UNPAID','PARTIAL','PAID')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_payment_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "cancel_reason_code IS NULL OR cancel_reason_code IN "
            "('PATIENT_REQUEST','MEDICAL','SCHEDULE','EQUIPMENT','WEATHER',"
            "'FINANCE','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_cancel_code"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'CANCELLED' OR (cancel_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(cancel_reason)) >= 5 "
            "AND cancel_reason_code IS NOT NULL "
            "AND cancelled_by IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_cancelled"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'POSTPONED' OR (postpone_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(postpone_reason)) >= 5)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_postponed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'NO_SHOW' OR no_show_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_noshow"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'SURGERY_DONE' OR (surgery_completed_at IS NOT NULL "
            "AND surgery_outcome_snap IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_surgery_done"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'IN_SURGERY' OR actual_start_time IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_resv_in_surgery"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status NOT IN ('READY','IN_SURGERY') OR (consent_completed = TRUE "
            "AND preop_med_completed = TRUE AND education_completed = TRUE "
            "AND checkin_completed = TRUE)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_ready_checklist"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "consent_completed = FALSE OR consent_completed_at IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_resv_consent_at"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "anest_type IS NULL OR anest_type NOT IN ('SEDATION','GENERAL') "
            "OR status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','CANCELLED','POSTPONED') "
            "OR fasting_confirmed = TRUE",
            name="ck_m11_resv_fasting"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status NOT IN ('SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','PREPARING','READY',"
            "'IN_SURGERY','SURGERY_DONE') OR m06_apply_id IS NOT NULL "
            "OR m06_sync_status IN ('FAILED','RETRYING')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_submitted"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status = 'DRAFT' OR (surgery_date IS NOT NULL AND surgeon_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_scheduled"),
        CheckConstraint("change_count >= 0", name="ck_m11_resv_change_count"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(plan_amount_snap IS NULL OR plan_amount_snap >= 0) "
            "AND (deposit_paid_snap IS NULL OR deposit_paid_snap >= 0) "
            "AND (total_paid_snap IS NULL OR total_paid_snap >= 0) "
            "AND (deposit_paid_snap IS NULL OR total_paid_snap IS NULL "
            "OR deposit_paid_snap <= total_paid_snap)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_amounts"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "actual_end_time IS NULL OR actual_start_time IS NULL "
            "OR actual_end_time >= actual_start_time",
            name="ck_m11_resv_actual_time_order"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "actual_start_time IS NULL OR actual_start_time::date >= surgery_date",
            name="ck_m11_resv_actual_start"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "planned_end_time IS NULL OR planned_start_time IS NULL "
            "OR planned_end_time >= planned_start_time",
            name="ck_m11_resv_planned_time_order"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "parent_reservation_no IS NULL OR parent_reservation_no <> reservation_no",
            name="ck_m11_resv_parent_self"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "followup_generated = FALSE OR followup_plan_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_resv_followup"),
    )

    medications: Mapped[List["M11ReservationMedication"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="reservation", lazy="selectin")
    educations: Mapped[List["M11ReservationEducation"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="reservation", lazy="selectin")
    consents: Mapped[List["M11Consent"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="reservation", lazy="selectin")
    checklists: Mapped[List["M11ReservationChecklist"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="reservation", lazy="selectin")
    changes: Mapped[List["M11ReservationChange"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="reservation", lazy="selectin")

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        """调试输出。"""
        return (f"<M11Reservation {self.reservation_no} {self.refractive_type} "
                f"{self.eye_side} {self.status}>")
```

```python
# app/models/m11/reservation_detail.py
"""M11-05 术前准备明细 ORM 模型：用药、宣教、同意书、核对、停台改期"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity, Index,
    Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, Text, func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.models.base import Base


class M11ReservationMedication(Base):
    """术前用药执行表：每次点药一条。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_medication"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reservation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    drug_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, doc="用药方案序号，从 1 起")
    drug_category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    drug_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    drug_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    drug_spec: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)
    usage_route: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="EYE_DROP", server_default="EYE_DROP")
    dose_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, doc="同一用药方案内的点药次序")
    dose_quantity: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=True)
    dose_unit: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    planned_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    actual_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    interval_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    interval_required_min: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    interval_ok: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    exec_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING", server_default="PENDING")
    exec_mode: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    exec_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    exec_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    exec_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    skip_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False, doc="用药眼别，术日用药限术眼，禁 NA")
    adverse_reaction: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    has_adverse: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    verify_photo_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    device_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_resv_med_dose", "reservation_id", "drug_seq",
              "eye_side", "dose_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_med_reservation", "reservation_id",
              "drug_seq", "dose_no"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_med_pending", "planned_time",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND exec_status = 'PENDING'")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_med_missed", "planned_time",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND exec_status = 'PENDING' "
                  "AND planned_time < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_med_patient", "patient_id", text("actual_time DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint(
            "drug_category IN ('ANTIBIOTIC','MYDRIATIC','MIOTIC','ANESTHETIC',"
            "'ANTI_INFLAMMATORY','LUBRICANT','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_category"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "usage_route IN ('EYE_DROP','EYE_OINTMENT','ORAL','INJECTION','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_route"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exec_status IN ('PENDING','DONE','SKIPPED','MISSED','REFUSED')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exec_mode IS NULL OR exec_mode IN ('HOSPITAL','SELF','FAMILY')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_mode"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exec_role IS NULL OR exec_role IN ('NURSE','PATIENT','FAMILY','DOCTOR')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_role"),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU')",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_med_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint("drug_seq >= 1 AND dose_no >= 1",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_med_seq"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exec_status <> 'DONE' OR (actual_time IS NOT NULL "
            "AND exec_role IS NOT NULL AND exec_mode IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_done"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exec_mode IS DISTINCT FROM 'HOSPITAL' "
            "OR (exec_by IS NOT NULL AND exec_by_name IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_hospital"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exec_status NOT IN ('SKIPPED','MISSED','REFUSED') "
            "OR (skip_reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(skip_reason)) >= 2)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_skip_reason"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "has_adverse = FALSE OR (adverse_reaction IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(adverse_reaction)) >= 2)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_adverse"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "interval_ok IS NULL OR interval_minutes IS NULL "
            "OR interval_required_min IS NULL "
            "OR interval_ok = (interval_minutes >= interval_required_min)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_interval"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(dose_quantity IS NULL AND dose_unit IS NULL) "
            "OR (dose_quantity IS NOT NULL AND dose_unit IS NOT NULL "
            "AND dose_quantity > 0)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_med_quantity"),
    )

    reservation: Mapped["M11Reservation"] = relationship(
        back_populates="medications")


class M11ReservationEducation(Base):
    """术前宣教记录表：记录宣教执行与患者理解确认。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_education"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reservation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    education_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    education_method: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="VERBAL", server_default="VERBAL")
    content_version: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    content_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    material_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    planned_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    conducted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    duration_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    educator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    educator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    educator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    understanding_level: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True, doc="患者理解程度 FULL/PARTIAL/POOR/UNABLE")
    reeducated: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    reeducate_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    patient_confirm_sign: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    patient_confirm_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    guardian_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    guardian_relation: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    guardian_sign: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    interpreter_used: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    interpreter_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    quiz_score: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    quiz_passed: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    completed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_resv_edu_type", "reservation_id", "education_type",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_edu_reservation", "reservation_id", "education_type"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_edu_pending", "planned_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND completed = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_edu_poor", "conducted_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND understanding_level IN ('POOR','UNABLE') "
                  "AND completed = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint(
            "education_type IN ('PREOP_RISK','INTRAOP_COOPERATION','POSTOP_CARE',"
            "'MEDICATION_GUIDE','REHAB_ACTIVITY','EMERGENCY_HANDLE','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "education_method IN ('VERBAL','VIDEO','LEAFLET','WECHAT','INTERACTIVE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_method"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "understanding_level IS NULL OR understanding_level IN "
            "('FULL','PARTIAL','POOR','UNABLE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_understanding"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "educator_role IS NULL OR educator_role IN ('NURSE','DOCTOR','CONSULTANT')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_role"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "guardian_relation IS NULL OR guardian_relation IN "
            "('PARENT','SPOUSE','CHILD','SIBLING','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_relation"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "completed = FALSE OR (conducted_at IS NOT NULL "
            "AND educator_id IS NOT NULL AND understanding_level IS NOT NULL "
            "AND patient_confirm_at IS NOT NULL AND completed_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_completed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "conducted_at IS NULL OR (educator_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND educator_name IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_executor"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "understanding_level IS DISTINCT FROM 'UNABLE' "
            "OR (guardian_sign IS NOT NULL OR interpreter_used = TRUE)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_unable"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "guardian_sign IS NULL OR (guardian_name IS NOT NULL "
            "AND guardian_relation IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_guardian"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "interpreter_used = FALSE OR interpreter_name IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_interpreter"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "quiz_score IS NULL OR (quiz_score >= 0 AND quiz_score <= 100)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_quiz"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "reeducate_count >= 0 AND (reeducated = FALSE OR reeducate_count > 0)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_reedu"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "duration_minutes IS NULL OR (duration_minutes >= 1 "
            "AND duration_minutes <= 240)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_edu_duration"),
    )

    reservation: Mapped["M11Reservation"] = relationship(
        back_populates="educations")


class M11Consent(Base):
    """知情同意书表：签署记录不可篡改、不可物理删除。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_consent"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reservation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    consent_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=False, doc="同意书编号 TYS+YYYYMMDD+6位")
    consent_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    template_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    template_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    consent_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=False, doc="同意书内容版本号")
    content_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=False, doc="同意书内容 SHA-256 指纹，防篡改")
    content_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        Text, nullable=True, doc="同意书正文快照")
    content_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    html_snapshot_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    sign_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING", server_default="PENDING")
    required_signers: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=2, server_default=text("2"))
    signed_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))

    # ---------- 患者签署 ----------
    patient_sign: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    patient_sign_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    patient_sign_ip: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    patient_sign_device: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(128), nullable=True)
    patient_sign_method: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    patient_fingerprint: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(128), nullable=True)
    patient_face_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 医师签署 ----------
    doctor_sign: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    doctor_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    doctor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    doctor_sign_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 见证人与监护人 ----------
    witness_sign: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    witness_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    witness_relation: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    witness_sign_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    guardian_sign: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    guardian_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    guardian_relation: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    guardian_sign_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    proxy_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    proxy_authorization_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 【核心安全组】同意书确认内容 ----------
    eye_side_confirmed: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=True, doc="同意书确认的眼别，须与预约一致")
    refractive_type_confirmed: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=True)
    procedure_risk_acknowledged: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    alternative_acknowledged: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    implant_model_confirmed: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(128), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 有效期与撤销 ----------
    valid_from: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    valid_to: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    expired_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    revoke_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    revoke_reason_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    revoked_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    revoked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    refuse_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 防篡改与归档 ----------
    verify_log: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    tamper_checked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    tamper_passed: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=True)
    archive_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    archived_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_consent_no", "consent_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_consent_active", "reservation_id", "consent_type",
              unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND sign_status IN "
                  "('PENDING','SIGNING','SIGNED')")),
        Index("idx_m11_consent_reservation", "reservation_id", "consent_type"),
        Index("idx_m11_consent_pending", "valid_to",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND sign_status IN ('PENDING','SIGNING')")),
        Index("idx_m11_consent_expiring", "valid_to",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND sign_status = 'SIGNED'")),
        Index("idx_m11_consent_patient", "patient_id", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_consent_hash", "content_hash",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint(r"consent_no ~ '^TYS[0-9]{14}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_consent_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "consent_type IN ('SURGERY','ANESTHESIA','IMPLANT','IOL','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_status IN ('PENDING','SIGNING','SIGNED','REVOKED','EXPIRED','REFUSED')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "patient_sign_method IS NULL OR patient_sign_method IN "
            "('HANDWRITTEN','ELECTRONIC','SMS_OTP','WET_INK')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_sign_method"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "witness_relation IS NULL OR witness_relation IN "
            "('FAMILY','NURSE','STAFF','NOTARY')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_witness"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "guardian_relation IS NULL OR guardian_relation IN "
            "('PARENT','SPOUSE','CHILD','SIBLING','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_guardian_relation"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "revoke_reason_code IS NULL OR revoke_reason_code IN "
            "('PATIENT_WITHDRAW','CONTENT_CHANGED','PLAN_CHANGED','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_revoke_code"),
        CheckConstraint(r"content_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_consent_hash_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "eye_side_confirmed IS NULL OR eye_side_confirmed IN ('OD','OS','OU')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refractive_type_confirmed IS NULL OR refractive_type_confirmed IN "
            "('SMILE','FS_LASIK','TRANSPRK','SMART','ICL','PRL','CXL','PIOL','RK',"
            "'OTHER_REFRACTIVE')",
            name="ck_m11_consent_refractive_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_status <> 'SIGNED' OR (signed_count >= required_signers "
            "AND patient_sign IS NOT NULL AND patient_sign_at IS NOT NULL "
            "AND patient_sign_method IS NOT NULL AND doctor_sign IS NOT NULL "
            "AND doctor_sign_at IS NOT NULL AND valid_from IS NOT NULL "
            "AND valid_to IS NOT NULL AND valid_to > valid_from)",
            name="ck_m11_consent_signed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_status <> 'SIGNED' OR (eye_side_confirmed IS NOT NULL "
            "AND refractive_type_confirmed IS NOT NULL "
            "AND procedure_risk_acknowledged = TRUE "
            "AND alternative_acknowledged = TRUE)",
            name="ck_m11_consent_acknowledged"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_status <> 'REVOKED' OR (revoke_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(revoke_reason)) >= 4 "
            "AND revoke_reason_code IS NOT NULL AND revoked_by IS NOT NULL "
            "AND revoked_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_consent_revoked"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_status <> 'REFUSED' OR (refuse_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(refuse_reason)) >= 2)",
            name="ck_m11_consent_refused"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "proxy_flag = FALSE OR proxy_authorization_url IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_consent_proxy"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(witness_sign IS NULL AND witness_name IS NULL) "
            "OR (witness_sign IS NOT NULL AND witness_name IS NOT NULL "
            "AND witness_relation IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_consent_witness_pair"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(guardian_sign IS NULL AND guardian_name IS NULL) "
            "OR (guardian_sign IS NOT NULL AND guardian_name IS NOT NULL "
            "AND guardian_relation IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_consent_guardian_pair"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "required_signers >= 1 AND signed_count >= 0 "
            "AND signed_count <= required_signers",
            name="ck_m11_consent_signers"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "consent_type NOT IN ('IMPLANT','IOL') OR sign_status <> 'SIGNED' "
            "OR implant_model_confirmed IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_consent_implant"),
    )

    reservation: Mapped["M11Reservation"] = relationship(
        back_populates="consents")


class M11ReservationChecklist(Base):
    """术前核对与交接表：眼别不一致必须硬阻断（BLOCKED）。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_checklist"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reservation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    check_round: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, doc="WARD/OR_ENTRY/TIME_OUT/PRE_DISCHARGE")
    check_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    check_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    check_result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING", server_default="PENDING")

    # ---------- 【核心安全组】眼别四处比对 ----------
    eye_side_reservation: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False, doc="预约眼别（核对基准）")
    eye_side_plan: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    eye_side_consent: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=True)
    eye_side_verified: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=True, doc="本次实际核实到的眼别")
    eye_side_match: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=True, doc="四处眼别是否全链一致")
    eye_side_mismatch_detail: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    laterality_mark_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    # ---------- 身份与七对 ----------
    identity_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    identity_method: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=True)
    wristband_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    patient_name_confirmed: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    patient_birth_confirmed: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(
        Date, nullable=True)
    check_seven_items: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True, doc="七对明细：name/gender/age/id_card/operation/eye_side/anesthesia")
    seven_items_all_passed: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=True)
    failed_items: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)

    # ---------- 其他核实项 ----------
    consent_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    consent_expired_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    medication_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    education_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    fasting_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    lab_result_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    implant_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    equipment_qualified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    preop_photo_taken: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    jewelry_removed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    makeup_removed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    # ---------- 交接 ----------
    handover_from_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
```
