```python
    handover_from_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    handover_to_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    handover_to_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    handover_time: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    handover_items: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 执行人与参与人 ----------
    executor_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    executor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    executor_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    participant_ids: Mapped[Optional[List[int]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True, doc="Time-Out 参与人员 ID 数组")
    participant_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    surgeon_present: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    patient_response_ok: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    # ---------- 硬阻断 ----------
    blocked_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    blocked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    blocked_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    unblock_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    unblock_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    unblock_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    unblock_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    signature_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    photo_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_resv_chk_round", "reservation_id", "check_round",
              "check_seq", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chk_reservation", "reservation_id",
              "check_round", "check_seq"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chk_eye_mismatch", "check_time",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND eye_side_match = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chk_blocked", "blocked_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND check_result = 'BLOCKED'")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chk_pending", "check_round",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND check_result = 'PENDING'")),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_round IN ('WARD','OR_ENTRY','TIME_OUT','PRE_DISCHARGE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_round"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_result IN ('PENDING','PASSED','FAILED','BLOCKED','SKIPPED')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_result"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "eye_side_reservation IN ('OD','OS','OU') "
            "AND (eye_side_plan IS NULL OR eye_side_plan IN ('OD','OS','OU')) "
            "AND (eye_side_consent IS NULL OR eye_side_consent IN ('OD','OS','OU')) "
            "AND (eye_side_verified IS NULL "
            "OR eye_side_verified IN ('OD','OS','OU'))",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_eye_values"),
        CheckConstraint("check_result <> 'PASSED' OR eye_side_match = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_chk_passed_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_result <> 'PASSED' OR seven_items_all_passed = TRUE",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_passed_seven"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_result <> 'PASSED' OR (identity_verified = TRUE "
            "AND consent_verified = TRUE AND medication_verified = TRUE "
            "AND education_verified = TRUE AND laterality_mark_verified = TRUE)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_passed_items"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "eye_side_match IS NULL OR eye_side_verified IS NULL "
            "OR (eye_side_match = (eye_side_reservation = eye_side_verified "
            "AND (eye_side_plan IS NULL "
            "OR eye_side_plan = eye_side_reservation) "
            "AND (eye_side_consent IS NULL "
            "OR eye_side_consent = eye_side_reservation)))",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_eye_consistent"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "seven_items_all_passed IS NULL OR check_seven_items IS NULL "
            "OR seven_items_all_passed = ("
            "(check_seven_items ->> 'name')::boolean "
            "AND (check_seven_items ->> 'gender')::boolean "
            "AND (check_seven_items ->> 'age')::boolean "
            "AND (check_seven_items ->> 'id_card')::boolean "
            "AND (check_seven_items ->> 'operation')::boolean "
            "AND (check_seven_items ->> 'eye_side')::boolean "
            "AND (check_seven_items ->> 'anesthesia')::boolean)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_seven_consistent"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_result = 'PENDING' OR (check_time IS NOT NULL "
            "AND executor_id IS NOT NULL AND executor_name IS NOT NULL "
            "AND executor_role IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_done"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_round <> 'TIME_OUT' OR check_result = 'PENDING' "
            "OR (surgeon_present = TRUE AND participant_count >= 3 "
            "AND participant_ids IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_timeout"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_round <> 'OR_ENTRY' OR check_result = 'PENDING' "
            "OR (handover_from_id IS NOT NULL AND handover_to_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND handover_from_id <> handover_to_id "
            "AND handover_time IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_handover"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_result <> 'BLOCKED' OR (blocked_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(blocked_reason)) >= 4 "
            "AND blocked_at IS NOT NULL AND blocked_by IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_blocked"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_result <> 'FAILED' OR failed_items IS NOT NULL "
            "OR eye_side_match = FALSE",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_failed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "unblock_at IS NULL OR (unblock_by IS NOT NULL "
            "AND unblock_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND (blocked_at IS NULL OR unblock_at >= blocked_at))",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_unblock"),
        CheckConstraint("check_seq >= 1", name="ck_m11_resv_chk_seq"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "participant_count IS NULL OR (participant_count >= 0 "
            "AND participant_count <= 30)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chk_participant"),
    )

    reservation: Mapped["M11Reservation"] = relationship(
        back_populates="checklists")
```

```python
# app/models/m11/reservation_track.py
"""M11-05 停台改期、状态流转、日汇总、审计日志 ORM 模型"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity, Index,
    Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.models.base import Base


class M11ReservationChange(Base):
    """停台与改期表：记录每次取消/改期的决策链。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_change"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    change_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=False, doc="变更单号 BG+YYYYMMDD+4位")
    reservation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    change_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, doc="该预约第几次变更，从 1 起")
    change_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)

    original_surgery_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(
        Date, nullable=True)
    original_surgeon_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    original_surgeon_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    original_room_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    original_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)

    new_surgery_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    new_surgeon_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    new_surgeon_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    new_room_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    new_reservation_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    delay_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)

    reason_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    reason_detail: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=False)
    reason_category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="OTHER", server_default="OTHER")
    is_hospital_side: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    initiator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    initiator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    initiator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    requested_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    approval_required: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    approval_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    approver_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    approver_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    approved_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    reject_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    m06_cancel_pushed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    m06_cancel_pushed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    m06_cancel_error: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    slot_released: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    slot_released_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---------- 金额快照（M16 提供，M11 不计算） ----------
    refund_required: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    refund_apply_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    refund_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 4), nullable=True, doc="退款金额快照，M16 提供")
    refund_status_snap: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    penalty_flag: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    penalty_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 4), nullable=True, doc="违约金快照，M16 提供")

    patient_notified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    notify_channel: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    notified_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    notify_message_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    notify_failed_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(256), nullable=True)
    new_consent_required: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    medical_record_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(1024), nullable=True)
    affect_quality_stat: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=True, server_default="TRUE")
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_resv_chg_no", "change_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_resv_chg_seq", "reservation_id", "change_seq", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chg_reservation", "reservation_id", "change_seq"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chg_date", "original_surgery_date", "change_type",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chg_hospital", "original_surgery_date",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND is_hospital_side = TRUE "
                  "AND change_type IN ('CANCEL','ABORT_IN_OR')")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chg_approval", "approval_status", "requested_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND approval_status = 'PENDING'")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chg_push", "m06_cancel_pushed",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND change_type IN ('CANCEL','POSTPONE') "
                  "AND m06_cancel_pushed = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_chg_new", "new_reservation_no",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "new_reservation_no IS NOT NULL AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint(r"change_no ~ '^BG[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_chg_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "change_type IN ('CANCEL','POSTPONE','NO_SHOW','ABORT_IN_OR',"
            "'REASSIGN','CHANGE_ROOM')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "reason_code IN ('PATIENT_REQUEST','PATIENT_ILLNESS','MEDICAL',"
            "'SCHEDULE_CONFLICT','EQUIPMENT_FAILURE','WEATHER','FINANCE',"
            "'FAMILY_ISSUE','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_reason_code"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "reason_category IN ('PATIENT','MEDICAL','RESOURCE','EXTERNAL','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_reason_category"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "initiator_role IS NULL OR initiator_role IN "
            "('PATIENT','FAMILY','DOCTOR','NURSE','ADMIN','SYSTEM')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_initiator"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "approval_status IS NULL OR approval_status IN "
            "('PENDING','APPROVED','REJECTED','NOT_REQUIRED')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_approval_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refund_status_snap IS NULL OR refund_status_snap IN "
            "('NONE','PENDING','DONE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_refund_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "notify_channel IS NULL OR notify_channel IN "
            "('SMS','WECHAT','PHONE','APP')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_notify_channel"),
        CheckConstraint("change_seq >= 1", name="ck_m11_resv_chg_seq_positive"),
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(reason_detail)) >= 5",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_chg_reason_detail"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "change_type <> 'POSTPONE' OR (new_surgery_date IS NOT NULL "
            "AND new_reservation_no IS NOT NULL "
            "AND original_surgery_date IS NOT NULL "
            "AND new_surgery_date > original_surgery_date "
            "AND delay_days IS NOT NULL AND delay_days > 0)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_postpone"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "change_type <> 'REASSIGN' OR (new_surgeon_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND new_surgeon_id <> original_surgeon_id)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_reassign"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "change_type <> 'CHANGE_ROOM' OR (new_room_code IS NOT NULL "
            "AND new_room_code <> original_room_code)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_room"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "approval_required = FALSE OR (approval_no IS NOT NULL "
            "AND approval_status IS NOT NULL AND approval_status <> 'PENDING')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_approval_no"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "approval_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'APPROVED' "
            "OR (approver_id IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_approved"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "approval_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'REJECTED' "
            "OR (reject_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(reject_reason)) >= 4)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_rejected"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refund_required = FALSE OR (refund_amount_snap IS NOT NULL "
            "AND refund_amount_snap >= 0 AND refund_status_snap IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_refund"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "penalty_flag = FALSE OR (penalty_amount_snap IS NOT NULL "
            "AND penalty_amount_snap >= 0)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_penalty"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "patient_notified = FALSE OR (notified_at IS NOT NULL "
            "AND notify_channel IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_notified"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "notify_failed_reason IS NULL OR patient_notified = FALSE",
            name="ck_m11_resv_chg_notify_fail"),
        CheckConstraint("delay_days IS NULL OR delay_days >= 0",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_chg_delay"),
    )

    reservation: Mapped["M11Reservation"] = relationship(
        back_populates="changes")


class M11ReservationStatusLog(Base):
    """预约状态流转日志：按月分区，记录每次状态变化与停留时长。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_status_log"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_m11_resv_stlog_reservation", "reservation_id", "operated_at"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_stlog_status", "to_status", "operated_at"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_stlog_stuck", "operated_at",
              postgresql_where=text("dwell_level = 'STUCK'")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_stlog_operator", "operator_id", "operated_at"),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_stlog_source", "transition_source", "operated_at"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "to_status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','PREPARING','READY',"
            "'IN_SURGERY','SURGERY_DONE','CANCELLED','POSTPONED','NO_SHOW')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_to_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "from_status IS NULL OR from_status IN "
            "('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','PREPARING','READY',"
            "'IN_SURGERY','SURGERY_DONE','CANCELLED','POSTPONED','NO_SHOW')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_from_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "transition_action IN ('CREATE','SUBMIT','SCHEDULE','START_PREP',"
            "'PREP_COMPLETE','START_SURGERY','COMPLETE','CANCEL','POSTPONE',"
            "'NO_SHOW')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_action"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "transition_source IN ('USER','M06','M09','EVENT','SYSTEM','API')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_source"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "dwell_level IS NULL OR dwell_level IN ('FAST','SLOW','STUCK')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_dwell"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "operator_role IS NULL OR operator_role IN "
            "('DOCTOR','NURSE','ADMIN','SYSTEM','API')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_role"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "from_status IS NULL OR from_status <> to_status",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_changed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(transition_action = 'CREATE' AND from_status IS NULL) "
            "OR (transition_action <> 'CREATE' AND from_status IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_create"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "dwell_seconds IS NULL OR dwell_seconds >= 0",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_dwell_sec"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "eye_side_snapshot IS NULL OR eye_side_snapshot IN ('OD','OS','OU')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_stlog_eye"),
        {
            "postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (operated_at)",
            "comment": "预约状态流转日志：按月分区，保留 36 个月",
        },
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reservation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    reservation_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    from_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    to_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    transition_action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    transition_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, default="USER", server_default="USER")
    dwell_seconds: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=True, doc="在原状态停留的秒数")
    dwell_level: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    status_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    change_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    eye_side_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=True, doc="变更时的眼别快照，用于眼别纵向审计")
    related_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    operator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    operated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        doc="状态变更时间，分区键")
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())


class M11ReservationDaily(Base):
    """手术日看板日汇总：按日期×医师×手术间预聚合。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_daily"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    summary_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    surgeon_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    surgeon_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    room_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    facility_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    dim_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="DETAIL", server_default="DETAIL")

    planned_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    completed_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    cancelled_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    postponed_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    no_show_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    in_progress_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    pending_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    completion_rate: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True, doc="完成率百分比，服务层计算")
    cancel_rate: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    no_show_rate: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    hospital_cancel_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))

    eye_side_od_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    eye_side_os_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    eye_side_ou_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    type_smile_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    type_fs_lasik_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    type_transprk_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    type_icl_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    type_other_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))

    ready_on_time_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    on_time_rate: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    avg_prep_minutes: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(8, 2), nullable=True)
    avg_turnover_minutes: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(8, 2), nullable=True)
    avg_surgery_minutes: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(8, 2), nullable=True)
    max_delay_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)

    checklist_blocked_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    eye_mismatch_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"),
        doc="眼别不一致发现次数，安全监控核心指标")
    consent_missing_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    med_incomplete_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))

    refresh_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="HOURLY", server_default="HOURLY")
    last_refreshed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        onupdate=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_resv_daily_dim", "summary_date", "dim_level",
              func.coalesce(text("surgeon_id"), 0),
              func.coalesce(text("room_code"), ""),
              unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_daily_date", text("summary_date DESC"), "dim_level",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_daily_surgeon", "surgeon_id",
              text("summary_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND surgeon_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_daily_room", "room_code", text("summary_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND room_code IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_daily_safety", text("summary_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND (eye_mismatch_count > 0 "
                  "OR checklist_blocked_count > 0)")),
        CheckConstraint(
            "dim_level IN ('DETAIL','SURGEON','ROOM','TOTAL')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_dim"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refresh_mode IN ('HOURLY','NIGHTLY','MANUAL')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_refresh"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "planned_count >= 0 AND completed_count >= 0 AND cancelled_count >= 0 "
            "AND postponed_count >= 0 AND no_show_count >= 0 "
            "AND in_progress_count >= 0 AND pending_count >= 0 "
            "AND hospital_cancel_count >= 0 AND eye_side_od_count >= 0 "
            "AND eye_side_os_count >= 0 AND eye_side_ou_count >= 0 "
            "AND type_smile_count >= 0 AND type_fs_lasik_count >= 0 "
            "AND type_transprk_count >= 0 AND type_icl_count >= 0 "
            "AND type_other_count >= 0 AND ready_on_time_count >= 0 "
            "AND checklist_blocked_count >= 0 AND eye_mismatch_count >= 0 "
            "AND consent_missing_count >= 0 AND med_incomplete_count >= 0",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_counts"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "completed_count + cancelled_count + postponed_count + no_show_count "
            "+ in_progress_count + pending_count <= planned_count",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_sum"),
        CheckConstraint("completed_count <= planned_count",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_daily_completed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "eye_side_od_count + eye_side_os_count + eye_side_ou_count <= planned_count",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_eye_sum"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "type_smile_count + type_fs_lasik_count + type_transprk_count "
            "+ type_icl_count + type_other_count <= planned_count",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_type_sum"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "ready_on_time_count <= completed_count + in_progress_count",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_ontime"),
        CheckConstraint("hospital_cancel_count <= cancelled_count",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_daily_hospital_cancel"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(completion_rate IS NULL OR (completion_rate >= 0 AND completion_rate <= 100)) "
            "AND (cancel_rate IS NULL OR (cancel_rate >= 0 AND cancel_rate <= 100)) "
            "AND (no_show_rate IS NULL OR (no_show_rate >= 0 AND no_show_rate <= 100)) "
            "AND (on_time_rate IS NULL OR (on_time_rate >= 0 AND on_time_rate <= 100))",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_rates"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(avg_prep_minutes IS NULL OR avg_prep_minutes >= 0) "
            "AND (avg_turnover_minutes IS NULL OR avg_turnover_minutes >= 0) "
            "AND (avg_surgery_minutes IS NULL OR avg_surgery_minutes >= 0) "
            "AND (max_delay_minutes IS NULL OR max_delay_minutes >= 0)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_durations"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(dim_level <> 'DETAIL' OR (surgeon_id IS NOT NULL AND room_code IS NOT NULL)) "
            "AND (dim_level <> 'SURGEON' OR (surgeon_id IS NOT NULL AND room_code IS NULL)) "
            "AND (dim_level <> 'ROOM' OR (surgeon_id IS NULL AND room_code IS NOT NULL)) "
            "AND (dim_level <> 'TOTAL' OR (surgeon_id IS NULL AND room_code IS NULL))",
            name="ck_m11_resv_daily_detail"),
    )


class M11ReservationLog(Base):
    """操作审计日志：只追加，按月分区，覆盖写操作与敏感读操作。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_reservation_log"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_resv_log_no", "log_no", "operated_at", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_reservation", "reservation_id",
              text("operated_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_operator", "operator_id",
              text("operated_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_action", "action", text("operated_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_safety", text("operated_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_safety_related = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_sensitive", text("operated_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_sensitive = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_failed", "result", text("operated_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("result <> 'SUCCESS'")),
        Index("idx_m11_resv_log_trace", "trace_id",
              postgresql_where=text("trace_id IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint(r"log_no ~ '^LOG[0-9]{16}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_log_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action IN ("
            "'CREATE','UPDATE','SUBMIT','SCHEDULE_CONFIRM',"
            "'START_PREP','PREP_COMPLETE','START_SURGERY','COMPLETE',"
            "'CANCEL','POSTPONE','NO_SHOW','REASSIGN','CHANGE_ROOM',"
            "'EYE_SIDE_MARK','EYE_SIDE_CHANGE','EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH_BLOCK',"
            "'CHECKLIST_EXEC','CHECKLIST_PASS','CHECKLIST_FAIL','CHECKLIST_BLOCK',"
            "'CHECKLIST_UNBLOCK',"
            "'CONSENT_GENERATE','CONSENT_SIGN','CONSENT_REVOKE','CONSENT_EXPIRE',"
            "'CONSENT_TAMPER_CHECK',"
            "'MED_EXEC','MED_SKIP','MED_ADVERSE',"
            "'EDU_EXEC','EDU_CONFIRM',"
            "'M06_PUSH','M06_CANCEL_PUSH','P09_CALLBACK',"
            "'VIEW_SENSITIVE','VIEW_CONSENT','EXPORT','PRINT',"
            "'CONFIG_UPDATE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_action"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action_group IN ('BUSINESS','SAFETY','CONSENT','AUDIT','EXPORT','CONFIG')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_group"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "result IN ('SUCCESS','FAILED','REJECTED')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_result"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "client_type IS NULL OR client_type IN "
            "('WEB','PAD','MOBILE','API','BATCH')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_client"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "http_method IS NULL OR http_method IN "
            "('GET','POST','PUT','PATCH','DELETE')",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_method"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "result = 'SUCCESS' OR (error_code IS NOT NULL AND error_msg IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_failed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_sensitive = FALSE OR (change_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(change_reason)) >= 4)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_sensitive"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action <> 'EYE_SIDE_CHANGE' OR (is_safety_related = TRUE "
            "AND is_sensitive = TRUE AND approval_no IS NOT NULL "
            "AND changed_fields IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_eye_change"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action NOT IN ('EYE_SIDE_MISMATCH_BLOCK','CHECKLIST_BLOCK') "
            "OR is_safety_related = TRUE",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_eye_block"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action NOT IN ('UPDATE','EYE_SIDE_CHANGE','REASSIGN','CHANGE_ROOM') "
            "OR (changed_fields IS NOT NULL AND change_summary IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_changed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action IN ('VIEW_SENSITIVE','VIEW_CONSENT','EXPORT','PRINT',"
            "'CONFIG_UPDATE','SEARCH') OR reservation_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_resv_log_target"),
        CheckConstraint("affected_rows IS NULL OR affected_rows >= 0",
                        name="ck_m11_resv_log_affected"),
        {
            "postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (operated_at)",
            "comment": "操作审计日志：按月分区，保留 36 个月，只追加",
        },
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    log_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    reservation_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    reservation_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    patient_name_mask: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, doc="患者姓名掩码，日志不落明文")
    action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(48), nullable=False)
    action_group: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="BUSINESS", server_default="BUSINESS")
    api_path: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)
    http_method: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    changed_fields: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(1000), nullable=True)
    before_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True, doc="变更前快照，敏感字段已脱敏")
    after_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True, doc="变更后快照，敏感字段已脱敏")
    change_summary: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(1024), nullable=True)
    change_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    is_sensitive: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    is_safety_related: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE",
        doc="是否与手术安全相关（眼别/核对/同意书）")
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    operator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    operator_dept: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    operator_ip: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    client_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="SUCCESS", server_default="SUCCESS")
    error_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    error_msg: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    affected_rows: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=True)
    operated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp(),
        doc="操作时间，分区键")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
```

---

### 4.12 Pydantic Schema 定义

```python
# app/schemas/m11/reservation.py
"""M11-05 手术预约与术前准备 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""

import re
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

EyeSide = Literal["OD", "OS", "OU"]
RefractiveType = Literal[
    "SMILE", "FS_LASIK", "TRANSPRK", "SMART", "ICL", "PRL",
    "CXL", "PIOL", "RK", "OTHER_REFRACTIVE",
]
ReservationStatus = Literal[
    "DRAFT", "SUBMITTED", "CONFIRMED", "PREPARING", "READY",
    "IN_SURGERY", "SURGERY_DONE", "CANCELLED", "POSTPONED", "NO_SHOW",
]
AnestType = Literal["SURFACE", "LOCAL", "SEDATION", "GENERAL"]
TimeSlot = Literal["AM", "PM", "EVENING"]
PriorityFlag = Literal["NORMAL", "URGENT", "VIP"]
ReservationSource = Literal["HOSPITAL", "ONLINE", "PHONE", "REFERRAL", "RESCHEDULE"]
ChangeType = Literal["CANCEL", "POSTPONE", "NO_SHOW", "ABORT_IN_OR",
                     "REASSIGN", "CHANGE_ROOM"]
ChangeReasonCode = Literal[
    "PATIENT_REQUEST", "PATIENT_ILLNESS", "MEDICAL", "SCHEDULE_CONFLICT",
    "EQUIPMENT_FAILURE", "WEATHER", "FINANCE", "FAMILY_ISSUE", "OTHER",
]
CheckRound = Literal["WARD", "OR_ENTRY", "TIME_OUT", "PRE_DISCHARGE"]
CheckResult = Literal["PENDING", "PASSED", "FAILED", "BLOCKED", "SKIPPED"]
ConsentType = Literal["SURGERY", "ANESTHESIA", "IMPLANT", "IOL", "OTHER"]
ConsentStatus = Literal["PENDING", "SIGNING", "SIGNED", "REVOKED", "EXPIRED", "REFUSED"]
SignMethod = Literal["HANDWRITTEN", "ELECTRONIC", "SMS_OTP", "WET_INK"]
DrugCategory = Literal["ANTIBIOTIC", "MYDRIATIC", "MIOTIC", "ANESTHETIC",
                       "ANTI_INFLAMMATORY", "LUBRICANT", "OTHER"]
MedExecStatus = Literal["PENDING", "DONE", "SKIPPED", "MISSED", "REFUSED"]
EducationType = Literal["PREOP_RISK", "INTRAOP_COOPERATION", "POSTOP_CARE",
                        "MEDICATION_GUIDE", "REHAB_ACTIVITY", "EMERGENCY_HANDLE", "OTHER"]
UnderstandingLevel = Literal["FULL", "PARTIAL", "POOR", "UNABLE"]

RESERVATION_NO_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^YY[0-9]{12}$")
SEVEN_ITEM_KEYS = ["name", "gender", "age", "id_card", "operation", "eye_side",
                   "anesthesia"]


# ==================== 预约请求体 ====================

class ReservationCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """登记手术预约（P03 核心）。"""

    patient_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="患者 ID")
    plan_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="手术方案号，必须已审核")
    exam_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="术前检查单号")
    record_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="屈光档案号")
    optometry_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="验光单号")
    refractive_type: RefractiveType = Field(..., description="屈光术式")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field(..., description="手术眼别 OD/OS/OU，禁 NA")
    surgery_date: date = Field(..., description="手术日期")
    time_slot: TimeSlot = Field("AM", description="时段")
    anest_type: AnestType = Field("SURFACE", description="麻醉方式")
    surgeon_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="主刀医师 ID")
    assistant_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="助手医师 ID")
    anesthesiologist_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="麻醉医师 ID")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="执行科室 ID")
    priority_flag: PriorityFlag = Field("NORMAL", description="优先级")
    reservation_source: ReservationSource = Field("HOSPITAL", description="预约来源")
    consult_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="来源咨询单号")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")

    @field_validator("eye_side")
    @classmethod
    def check_eye_side(cls, v: str) -> str:
        """眼别只允许 OD/OS/OU。"""
        if v not in ("OD", "OS", "OU"):
            raise ValueError("眼别必须为 OD / OS / OU，不允许 NA 或其它值")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_refractive_eye(self) -> "ReservationCreateIn":
        """术式与眼别的组合校验。"""
        if self.refractive_type in ("CXL",) and self.eye_side == "OU":
            raise ValueError("CXL 交联手术必须指定单眼 OD 或 OS，不支持双眼同台")
        if self.priority_flag == "URGENT" and self.surgery_date < date.today():
            raise ValueError("加急预约的手术日期不得早于今天")
        return self


class ReservationUpdateIn(BaseModel):
    """更新预约（仅 DRAFT 状态可全量修改）。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    surgery_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="手术日期")
    time_slot: Optional[TimeSlot] = Field(None, description="时段")
    anest_type: Optional[AnestType] = Field(None, description="麻醉方式")
    surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主刀医师")
    assistant_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="助手医师")
    anesthesiologist_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="麻醉医师")
    priority_flag: Optional[PriorityFlag] = Field(None, description="优先级")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号，必传")


class EyeSideChangeIn(BaseModel):
    """变更眼别（高风险操作，必须审批）。"""

    new_eye_side: EyeSide = Field(..., description="新眼别")
    change_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512,
                               description="变更原因，≥5 字")
    approval_no: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64,
                             description="审批单号，必须有")
    plan_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="支撑新眼别的方案号")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_not_same(self) -> "EyeSideChangeIn":
        """新眼别必须与原眼别不同（由服务层比对）。"""
        if not self.change_reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("变更眼别必须填写具体原因")
        return self


class LateralityMarkIn(BaseModel):
    """术眼标记（患者身体上的物理标记）。"""

    laterality_mark: EyeSide = Field(..., description="标记眼别，必须与术眼一致")
    witness_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="见证人 ID，不能与执行人相同")
    photo_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="标记照片")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")


class ReservationSubmitIn(BaseModel):
    """提交并推送 M06。"""

    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    force_push: bool = Field(False, description="推送失败时是否强制重试")


class ReservationCancelIn(BaseModel):
    """取消/停台。"""

    reason_code: ChangeReasonCode = Field(..., description="取消原因编码")
    reason_detail: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512,
                               description="原因详细说明，≥5 字")
    is_hospital_side: bool = Field(False, description="是否院方原因")
    approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                       description="手术前 24 小时内停台必须审批")
    notify_patient: bool = Field(True, description="是否通知患者")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")


class ReservationPostponeIn(BaseModel):
    """改期（生成新预约）。"""

    new_surgery_date: date = Field(..., description="新手术日期，须晚于原日期")
    new_surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="新主刀医师")
    reason_code: ChangeReasonCode = Field(..., description="改期原因编码")
    reason_detail: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512,
                               description="原因详细说明，≥5 字")
    is_hospital_side: bool = Field(False, description="是否院方原因")
    approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="审批单号")
    notify_patient: bool = Field(True, description="是否通知患者")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")


class NoShowIn(BaseModel):
    """标记未到。"""

    reason_detail: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512,
                               description="未到情况说明")
    contact_attempts: int = Field(0, ge=0, le=10, description="联系尝试次数")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")


# ==================== 术前准备请求体 ====================

class MedicationPlanIn(BaseModel):
    """生成术前用药方案。"""

    drug_category: DrugCategory = Field(..., description="用药类别")
    drug_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="药品编码")
    drug_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=200, description="药品名称")
    usage_route: str = Field("EYE_DROP", description="给药途径")
    total_times: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=60, description="总点药次数")
    interval_required_min: int = Field(..., ge=0, le=1440,
                                       description="要求的最小间隔分钟")
    first_planned_time: datetime = Field(..., description="首次计划点药时间")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field(..., description="用药眼别")
    exec_mode: str = Field("HOSPITAL", description="执行方式 HOSPITAL/SELF/FAMILY")


class MedicationExecIn(BaseModel):
    """执行点药。"""

    drug_seq: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="用药方案序号")
    dose_no: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="点药次序")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field(..., description="用药眼别")
    actual_time: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="实际时间，缺省取当前")
    exec_mode: str = Field("HOSPITAL", description="执行方式")
    dose_quantity: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, gt=0, description="用量")
    dose_unit: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=16, description="用量单位")
    has_adverse: bool = Field(False, description="是否有不良反应")
    adverse_reaction: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="不良反应")
    verify_photo_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="自点照片")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_adverse(self) -> "MedicationExecIn":
        """有不良反应必须有描述。"""
        if self.has_adverse and not (self.adverse_reaction or "").strip():
            raise ValueError("标记有不良反应时必须填写不良反应描述")
        if self.eye_side == "OU":
            raise ValueError("术日用药必须明确到单眼，不允许 OU")
        return self


class MedicationSkipIn(BaseModel):
    """跳过/漏执行点药。"""

    drug_seq: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="用药方案序号")
    dose_no: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="点药次序")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field(..., description="用药眼别")
    exec_status: Literal["SKIPPED", "MISSED", "REFUSED"] = Field(
        ..., description="执行状态")
    skip_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=2, max_length=256, description="原因")


class EducationExecIn(BaseModel):
    """执行宣教。"""

    education_type: EducationType = Field(..., description="宣教类型")
    education_method: str = Field("VERBAL", description="宣教方式")
    conducted_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="执行时间")
    duration_minutes: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=240, description="时长")
    understanding_level: UnderstandingLevel = Field(..., description="患者理解程度")
    patient_confirm_sign: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                                description="患者确认签名")
    guardian_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="监护人姓名")
    guardian_relation: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="与患者关系")
    guardian_sign: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="监护人签名")
    interpreter_used: bool = Field(False, description="是否使用翻译")
    interpreter_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="翻译姓名")
    quiz_score: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="掌握度得分")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_unable(self) -> "EducationExecIn":
        """无法理解必须有监护人或翻译。"""
        if self.understanding_level == "UNABLE":
            if not self.guardian_sign and not self.interpreter_used:
                raise ValueError("患者无法理解时必须有监护人签名或使用翻译")
        if self.guardian_sign and not self.guardian_relation:
            raise ValueError("填写监护人签名时必须填写与患者关系")
        if self.interpreter_used and not self.interpreter_name:
            raise ValueError("使用翻译时必须填写翻译人员姓名")
        return self


# ==================== 知情同意请求体 ====================

class ConsentGenerateIn(BaseModel):
    """生成知情同意书。"""

    consent_type: ConsentType = Field(..., description="同意书类型")
    template_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="模板编码")
    eye_side_confirmed: EyeSide = Field(..., description="同意书确认眼别")
    refractive_type_confirmed: RefractiveType = Field(..., description="确认术式")
    implant_model: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=128,
                                         description="植入物型号（ICL/PRL）")
    required_signers: int = Field(2, ge=1, le=4, description="需要签署方数量")
    valid_hours: int = Field(72, ge=1, le=2160, description="有效期小时数")


class ConsentSignIn(BaseModel):
    """签署知情同意书。"""

    signer_role: Literal["PATIENT", "DOCTOR", "WITNESS", "GUARDIAN"] = Field(
        ..., description="签署人角色")
    sign_value: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64, description="签名值")
    sign_method: SignMethod = Field(..., description="签署方式")
    procedure_risk_acknowledged: bool = Field(False, description="已知悉手术风险")
    alternative_acknowledged: bool = Field(False, description="已知悉替代方案")
    proxy_flag: bool = Field(False, description="是否代理人签署")
    proxy_authorization_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                                   description="授权委托书")
    witness_relation: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="见证人身份")
    guardian_relation: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="监护关系")
    face_photo_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="人脸抓拍")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_patient_ack(self) -> "ConsentSignIn":
        """患者签署必须确认风险与替代方案。"""
        if self.signer_role == "PATIENT":
            if not self.procedure_risk_acknowledged:
                raise ValueError("患者签署前必须确认已知悉手术风险")
            if not self.alternative_acknowledged:
                raise ValueError("患者签署前必须确认已知悉替代方案")
        if self.proxy_flag and not self.proxy_authorization_url:
            raise ValueError("代理签署必须上传授权委托书")
        if self.signer_role == "WITNESS" and not self.witness_relation:
            raise ValueError("见证人签署必须填写见证人身份")
        if self.signer_role == "GUARDIAN" and not self.guardian_relation:
            raise ValueError("监护人签署必须填写与患者关系")
        return self


class ConsentRevokeIn(BaseModel):
    """撤销知情同意书。"""

    revoke_reason_code: Literal["PATIENT_WITHDRAW", "CONTENT_CHANGED",
                                "PLAN_CHANGED", "OTHER"] = Field(
        ..., description="撤销原因编码")
    revoke_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=4, max_length=512,
                               description="撤销原因说明")


# ==================== 术前核对请求体 ====================

class ChecklistExecIn(BaseModel):
    """执行术前核对（三查七对 + 眼别核对）。"""

    check_round: CheckRound = Field(..., description="核对轮次")
    eye_side_verified: EyeSide = Field(..., description="实际核实到的眼别")
    seven_items: Dict[str, bool] = Field(..., description="七对明细，7 个布尔项")
    identity_method: str = Field("WRISTBAND", description="身份核对方式")
    wristband_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="腕带编号")
    patient_name_confirmed: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                                  description="患者自述姓名")
    patient_birth_confirmed: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="患者自述出生日期")
    laterality_mark_verified: bool = Field(False, description="术眼标记已核实")
    consent_verified: bool = Field(False, description="同意书已核实")
    medication_verified: bool = Field(False, description="用药已完成")
    education_verified: bool = Field(False, description="宣教已完成")
    fasting_verified: bool = Field(False, description="禁食已核实")
    lab_result_verified: bool = Field(False, description="检验结果已核实")
    implant_verified: bool = Field(False, description="植入物已核实")
    equipment_qualified: bool = Field(False, description="设备在有效期内")
    preop_photo_taken: bool = Field(False, description="已拍术前照片")
    jewelry_removed: bool = Field(False, description="已摘除饰品")
    makeup_removed: bool = Field(False, description="已卸妆")
    participant_ids: Optional[List[int]] = Field(None, description="Time-Out 参与人")
    surgeon_present: bool = Field(False, description="主刀是否在场")
    patient_response_ok: bool = Field(False, description="患者能否正确应答")
    handover_to_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="接班人 ID")
    signature_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="核对单签名")
    photo_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="现场照片")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")

    @field_validator("seven_items")
    @classmethod
    def check_seven(cls, v: Dict[str, bool]) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """七对明细必须包含全部 7 个键。"""
        missing = [k for k in SEVEN_ITEM_KEYS if k not in v]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError("七对明细缺少项：%s" % ",".join(missing))
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_timeout(self) -> "ChecklistExecIn":
        """Time-Out 必须主刀在场且参与人员 >= 3。"""
        if self.check_round == "TIME_OUT":
            if not self.surgeon_present:
                raise ValueError("Time-Out 核对必须主刀医师在场")
            if not self.participant_ids or len(self.participant_ids) < 3:
                raise ValueError("Time-Out 核对必须至少 3 名在场人员共同确认")
        return self


class ChecklistUnblockIn(BaseModel):
    """解除核对硬阻断（必须审批）。"""

    unblock_approval_no: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64,
                                     description="审批单号，必须有")
    unblock_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=4, max_length=512,
                                description="解除原因，≥4 字")
    confirmed_eye_side: EyeSide = Field(..., description="确认后的最终眼别")
    confirm_by: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="确认人（科主任/主刀）ID")


# ==================== 内部契约请求体 ====================

class SurgeryCompletedIn(BaseModel):
    """P-09 手术完成回调（M06/M09 调用）。"""

    reservation_no: str = Field(..., description="手术预约号")
    surgery_completed_at: datetime = Field(..., description="术毕时间")
    surgery_outcome: Literal["COMPLETED", "ABORTED", "CONVERTED", "OTHER"] = Field(
        ..., description="术中结果")
    actual_start_time: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="实际开台时间")
    actual_end_time: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="实际下台时间")
    m09_surgery_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="M09 手术记录 ID")
    surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="实际主刀医师")
    eye_side_actual: Optional[EyeSide] = Field(
        None, description="实际手术眼别（用于与预约眼别比对）")
    intraop_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(
        None, description="术中摘要（激光参数/并发症等，M11 只读存储）")
    blood_loss_ml: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=0, description="出血量 ml")
    complications: Optional[List[str]] = Field(
        None, description="术中并发症编码列表")
    caller: str = Field(..., max_length=16, description="调用方模块 M06/M09")

    @field_validator("reservation_no")
    @classmethod
    def check_no(cls, v: str) -> str:
        """预约号格式校验。"""
        if not RESERVATION_NO_PATTERN.match(v):
            raise ValueError("预约号格式不正确，应为 YY+YYYYMMDD+4位")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_time_order(self) -> "SurgeryCompletedIn":
        """时间顺序与结果一致性。"""
        if self.actual_end_time and self.actual_start_time:
            if self.actual_end_time < self.actual_start_time:
                raise ValueError("实际下台时间不得早于实际开台时间")
        if self.surgery_outcome == "ABORTED":
            if not self.complications:
                raise ValueError("术中中止必须说明原因或并发症")
        return self

# ==================== 查询体 ====================

class ReservationQuery(BaseModel):
    """预约列表查询条件。"""

    surgery_date_from: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="手术日期起")
    surgery_date_to: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="手术日期止")
    status: Optional[List[ReservationStatus]] = Field(None, description="状态多选")
    refractive_type: Optional[List[RefractiveType]] = Field(None, description="术式多选")
    eye_side: Optional[List[EyeSide]] = Field(None, description="眼别多选")
    surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主刀医师")
    room_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="手术间")
    patient_keyword: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                           description="患者姓名/ID/预约号")
    pending_only: bool = Field(False, description="仅看准备未齐备的预约")
    ready_only: bool = Field(False, description="仅看可开台的预约")
    safety_flag: bool = Field(False, description="仅看有安全异常的预约")
    org_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="院区")
    page: int = Field(1, ge=1, description="页码")
    page_size: int = Field(20, ge=1, le=200, description="每页条数")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_span(self) -> "ReservationQuery":
        """日期跨度不得超过一年。"""
        if self.surgery_date_from and self.surgery_date_to:
            if self.surgery_date_from > self.surgery_date_to:
                raise ValueError("起始日期不得晚于截止日期")
            if (self.surgery_date_to - self.surgery_date_from).days > 366:
                raise ValueError("查询时间跨度不得超过一年")
        return self


class WorkbenchQuery(BaseModel):
    """手术预约工作台查询。"""

    work_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="工作日期，缺省今天")
    surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主刀医师")
    room_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="手术间")
    org_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="院区")


class BoardQuery(BaseModel):
    """手术日看板查询。"""

    board_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="看板日期，缺省今天")
    room_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="手术间")
    refresh_seconds: int = Field(30, ge=5, le=300, description="自动刷新间隔秒")


class ReservationChangeOut(BaseModel):
    """停台改期记录出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    change_no: str = Field(..., description="变更单号")
    change_seq: int = Field(..., description="第几次变更")
    change_type: ChangeType = Field(..., description="变更类型")
    original_surgery_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="原手术日期")
    new_surgery_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="新手术日期")
    new_reservation_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="新预约号")
    delay_days: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="延期天数")
    reason_code: ChangeReasonCode = Field(..., description="原因编码")
    reason_detail: str = Field(..., description="原因说明")
    is_hospital_side: bool = Field(..., description="是否院方原因")
    approval_status: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="审批状态")
    refund_amount_snap: Optional[Decimal] = Field(
        None, description="退款金额快照（M16 提供）")
    patient_notified: bool = Field(..., description="是否已通知患者")
    created_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="记录时间")


ReservationCreateIn.model_rebuild()
MedicationExecIn.model_rebuild()
ChecklistExecIn.model_rebuild()
ReservationDetailOut.model_rebuild()
ReservationInternalOut.model_rebuild()
```

## 附录 A：表清单速查

| 编号 | 表名 | 中文名 | 主键 | 主要唯一键 | 量级（年） | 分区 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4.2 | `m11_reservation` | 手术预约主表 | `id` | `reservation_no`；`(patient_id, surgery_date, eye_side)` 活跃唯一 | 4,800 | 否 |
| 4.3 | `m11_reservation_medication` | 术前用药执行表 | `id` | `(reservation_id, drug_seq, eye_side, dose_no)` | 43,200 | 否 |
| 4.4 | `m11_reservation_education` | 术前宣教记录表 | `id` | `(reservation_id, education_type)` | 9,600 | 否 |
| 4.5 | `m11_consent` | 知情同意书表 | `id` | `consent_no`；`(reservation_id, consent_type)` 活跃唯一 | 9,600 | 否 |
| 4.6 | `m11_reservation_checklist` | 术前核对与交接表 | `id` | `(reservation_id, check_round, check_seq)` | 14,400 | 否 |
| 4.7 | `m11_reservation_change` | 停台与改期表 | `id` | `change_no`；`(reservation_id, change_seq)` | 480 | 否 |
| 4.8 | `m11_reservation_status_log` | 预约状态流转日志表 | `(id, operated_at)` | `(reservation_id, operated_at)` 查询用 | 33,600 | **是**（月） |
| 4.9 | `m11_reservation_daily` | 手术日看板日汇总表 | `id` | `(summary_date, dim_level, surgeon_id, room_code)` | 3,120 | 否 |
| 4.10 | `m11_reservation_log` | 操作审计日志表 | `(id, operated_at)` | `(log_no, operated_at)` | 57,600 | **是**（月） |

**表间引用关系（应用层校验，无物理外键）：**

| 从表 | 字段 | 到表 | 基数 | 级联策略 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m11_reservation_medication` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | 主表逻辑删除时用药记录保留 |
| `m11_reservation_education` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | 同上 |
| `m11_consent` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | **永不删除**（法律文件） |
| `m11_reservation_checklist` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | **永不删除**（安全记录） |
| `m11_reservation_change` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | **永不删除**（质量原始数据） |
| `m11_reservation_status_log` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | 只追加，超期归档 |
| `m11_reservation_log` | `reservation_id` | `m11_reservation.id` | N:1 | 只追加，超期归档 |
| `m11_reservation` | `patient_id` | M03 `mpi_patient.id` | N:1 | 只读引用 |
| `m11_reservation` | `plan_no` | M11-04 `m11_surgery_plan` | N:1 | 只读引用（P-04） |
| `m11_reservation` | `exam_no` | M11-02 `m11_preop_exam` | N:1 | 只读引用（P-10） |
| `m11_reservation` | `record_no` | M11-03 `m11_refractive_record` | N:1 | 只读引用（P-02） |
| `m11_reservation` | `optometry_no` | M10-01 `m10_optometry` | N:1 | 只读引用（P-01） |
| `m11_reservation` | `m06_apply_id` | M06-01 `m06_day_apply` | N:1 | 只读引用 |
| `m11_reservation` | `m09_reception_id` | M09 手术麻醉接收 | N:1 | 只读引用 |
| `m11_reservation` | `followup_plan_no` | M11-06 `m11_postop_followup` | N:1 | 本模块下游 |
| `m11_reservation` | `parent_reservation_no` | `m11_reservation.reservation_no` | 自关联 | 改期链 |

## 附录 B：状态枚举汇总

| 枚举 | 字段 | 取值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **预约状态** | `m11_reservation.status` | `DRAFT` / `SUBMITTED` / `CONFIRMED` / `PREPARING` / `READY` / `IN_SURGERY` / `SURGERY_DONE` / `CANCELLED` / `POSTPONED` / `NO_SHOW` | 与共享规范 13 一字不差 |
| **眼别** | 多处 `eye_side` | `OD` / `OS` / `OU`（本子模块**禁 `NA`**） | 屈光手术眼别不可为空 |
| **屈光术式** | `m11_reservation.refractive_type` | `SMILE` / `FS_LASIK` / `TRANSPRK` / `SMART` / `ICL` / `PRL` / `CXL` / `PIOL` / `RK` / `OTHER_REFRACTIVE` | 与 M01-07 3.1.4 一字不差 |
| **麻醉方式** | `m11_reservation.anest_type` | `SURFACE` / `LOCAL` / `SEDATION` / `GENERAL` | 屈光以表面麻醉为主 |
| **时段** | `m11_reservation.time_slot` | `AM` / `PM` / `EVENING` | — |
| **优先级** | `m11_reservation.priority_flag` | `NORMAL` / `URGENT` / `VIP` | — |
| **预约来源** | `m11_reservation.reservation_source` | `HOSPITAL` / `ONLINE` / `PHONE` / `REFERRAL` / `RESCHEDULE` | — |
| **M06 推送状态** | `m11_reservation.m06_sync_status` | `NOT_PUSHED` / `PUSHED` / `FAILED` / `RETRYING` | — |
| **缴费状态快照** | `m11_reservation.payment_status_snap` | `UNPAID` / `PARTIAL` / `PAID` | M16 提供 |
| **取消原因编码** | `m11_reservation.cancel_reason_code` | `PATIENT_REQUEST` / `MEDICAL` / `SCHEDULE` / `EQUIPMENT` / `WEATHER` / `FINANCE` / `OTHER` | — |
| **术中结果快照** | `m11_reservation.surgery_outcome_snap` | `COMPLETED` / `ABORTED` / `CONVERTED` / `OTHER` | 来自 M09 事件 |
| **核对轮次** | `m11_reservation_checklist.check_round` | `WARD` / `OR_ENTRY` / `TIME_OUT` / `PRE_DISCHARGE` | 三查七对的轮次 |
| **核对结果** | `m11_reservation_checklist.check_result` | `PENDING` / `PASSED` / `FAILED` / `BLOCKED` / `SKIPPED` | `BLOCKED` 为硬阻断 |
| **同意书类型** | `m11_consent.consent_type` | `SURGERY` / `ANESTHESIA` / `IMPLANT` / `IOL` / `OTHER` | — |
| **同意书状态** | `m11_consent.sign_status` | `PENDING` / `SIGNING` / `SIGNED` / `REVOKED` / `EXPIRED` / `REFUSED` | — |
| **签署方式** | `m11_consent.patient_sign_method` | `HANDWRITTEN` / `ELECTRONIC` / `SMS_OTP` / `WET_INK` | — |
| **用药类别** | `m11_reservation_medication.drug_category` | `ANTIBIOTIC` / `MYDRIATIC` / `MIOTIC` / `ANESTHETIC` / `ANTI_INFLAMMATORY` / `LUBRICANT` / `OTHER` | — |
| **用药执行状态** | `m11_reservation_medication.exec_status` | `PENDING` / `DONE` / `SKIPPED` / `MISSED` / `REFUSED` | — |
| **宣教类型** | `m11_reservation_education.education_type` | `PREOP_RISK` / `INTRAOP_COOPERATION` / `POSTOP_CARE` / `MEDICATION_GUIDE` / `REHAB_ACTIVITY` / `EMERGENCY_HANDLE` / `OTHER` | 7 类 |
| **理解程度** | `m11_reservation_education.understanding_level` | `FULL` / `PARTIAL` / `POOR` / `UNABLE` | `UNABLE` 须有监护人或翻译 |
| **变更类型** | `m11_reservation_change.change_type` | `CANCEL` / `POSTPONE` / `NO_SHOW` / `ABORT_IN_OR` / `REASSIGN` / `CHANGE_ROOM` | — |
| **变更原因编码** | `m11_reservation_change.reason_code` | `PATIENT_REQUEST` / `PATIENT_ILLNESS` / `MEDICAL` / `SCHEDULE_CONFLICT` / `EQUIPMENT_FAILURE` / `WEATHER` / `FINANCE` / `FAMILY_ISSUE` / `OTHER` | — |
| **审批状态** | `m11_reservation_change.approval_status` | `PENDING` / `APPROVED` / `REJECTED` / `NOT_REQUIRED` | — |
| **流转来源** | `m11_reservation_status_log.transition_source` | `USER` / `M06` / `M09` / `EVENT` / `SYSTEM` / `API` | — |
| **停留分级** | `m11_reservation_status_log.dwell_level` | `FAST` / `SLOW` / `STUCK` | — |
| **汇总维度** | `m11_reservation_daily.dim_level` | `DETAIL` / `SURGEON` / `ROOM` / `TOTAL` | — |
| **审计操作结果** | `m11_reservation_log.result` | `SUCCESS` / `FAILED` / `REJECTED` | — |

**状态映射（与共享规范 13.1 一致）：**

| M11 `reservation.status` | M06 `apply.status` | M09 `reception.status` | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DRAFT` | — | — | 草稿，未推 M06 |
| `SUBMITTED` | `PENDING` | — | 已推 M06，待准入 |
| `CONFIRMED` | `APPROVED` | `PENDING` | 已排台 |
| `PREPARING` | `SCHEDULED` | `PENDING` | 术前准备中 |
| `READY` | `SCHEDULED` | `READY` | 可开台 |
| `IN_SURGERY` | `SCHEDULED` | `ANESTHESIA_STARTED` | 术中 |
| `SURGERY_DONE` | `COMPLETED` | `COMPLETED` | 术毕 |
| `CANCELLED` | `CANCELLED` | — | 已取消 |
| `POSTPONED` | `CANCELLED` | — | 已改期 |
| `NO_SHOW` | `CANCELLED` | — | 未到 |

## 附录 C：关键索引与查询

**C.1 手术日看板查询（P08，最高频）**

```sql
-- 当日全部预约，按手术间与台序排列
SELECT r.reservation_no, r.patient_name, r.refractive_type, r.eye_side,
       r.sequence_no, r.status, r.surgery_status_snap,
       r.consent_completed, r.preop_med_completed,
       r.education_completed, r.checkin_completed,
       r.planned_start_time, r.actual_start_time, r.actual_end_time,
       r.surgeon_name, r.room_code
FROM m11_reservation r
WHERE r.surgery_date = CURRENT_DATE
  AND r.is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY r.room_code, r.sequence_no NULLS LAST, r.planned_start_time;
-- 命中：idx_m11_reservation_date
-- 期望：P95 < 25ms（单日 15~25 台）
```

**C.2 P-07 取手术预约（内部契约）**

```sql
SELECT reservation_no, patient_id, patient_name, refractive_type, eye_side,
       laterality_mark, surgery_date, surgeon_id, plan_no, status,
       consent_completed, checkin_completed, admission_result_snap,
       surgery_status_snap
FROM m11_reservation
WHERE reservation_no = 'YY202610310005'
  AND is_deleted = FALSE;
-- 命中：uk_m11_reservation_no
-- 期望：P95 < 10ms
```

**C.3 P-08 按患者取预约清单**

```sql
SELECT reservation_no, surgery_date, refractive_type, eye_side, status,
       surgeon_name, followup_plan_no
FROM m11_reservation
WHERE patient_id = 10021
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY surgery_date DESC
LIMIT 20;
-- 命中：idx_m11_reservation_patient
-- 期望：P95 < 15ms
```

**C.4 术前准备未齐备清单（P01 工作台待办）**

```sql
SELECT reservation_no, patient_name, refractive_type, eye_side,
       surgery_date, planned_start_time,
       (4
        - (consent_completed)::int
        - (preop_med_completed)::int
        - (education_completed)::int
        - (checkin_completed)::int) AS missing_count
FROM m11_reservation
WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
  AND status IN ('CONFIRMED', 'PREPARING')
  AND surgery_date BETWEEN CURRENT_DATE AND CURRENT_DATE + 3
  AND (consent_completed = FALSE OR preop_med_completed = FALSE
       OR education_completed = FALSE OR checkin_completed = FALSE)
ORDER BY surgery_date, planned_start_time;
-- 命中：idx_m11_reservation_pending（部分索引）
-- 期望：P95 < 20ms
```

**C.5 眼别不一致清单（安全监控，质控每日必看）**

```sql
SELECT c.reservation_no, c.check_round, c.check_time,
       c.eye_side_reservation, c.eye_side_plan, c.eye_side_consent,
       c.eye_side_verified, c.eye_side_mismatch_detail,
       c.check_result, c.blocked_reason, c.unblock_approval_no
FROM m11_reservation_checklist c
WHERE c.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND c.eye_side_match = FALSE
  AND c.check_time >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '30 days'
ORDER BY c.check_time DESC;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_chk_eye_mismatch
-- 期望：P95 < 15ms
```

**C.6 硬阻断清单**

```sql
SELECT c.reservation_no, c.check_round, c.blocked_at, c.blocked_reason,
       c.unblock_approval_no, c.unblock_at,
       r.patient_name, r.eye_side, r.surgery_date
FROM m11_reservation_checklist c
JOIN m11_reservation r
  ON r.id = c.reservation_id AND r.is_deleted = FALSE
WHERE c.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND c.check_result = 'BLOCKED'
  AND c.unblock_at IS NULL
ORDER BY c.blocked_at DESC;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_chk_blocked（部分索引）
-- 期望：P95 < 10ms
```

**C.7 当日待点药清单（护士站）**

```sql
SELECT m.reservation_no, m.drug_category, m.drug_name, m.dose_no,
       m.planned_time, m.eye_side, m.exec_status,
       r.patient_name, r.eye_side AS surgery_eye
FROM m11_reservation_medication m
JOIN m11_reservation r
  ON r.id = m.reservation_id AND r.is_deleted = FALSE
WHERE m.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND m.exec_status = 'PENDING'
  AND m.planned_time::date = CURRENT_DATE
ORDER BY m.planned_time, m.reservation_no, m.drug_seq, m.dose_no;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_med_pending
-- 期望：P95 < 20ms
```

**C.8 同意书即将过期清单（定时任务）**

```sql
SELECT consent_no, reservation_no, consent_type, valid_to,
       patient_sign_at, signed_count, required_signers
FROM m11_consent
WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
  AND sign_status = 'SIGNED'
  AND valid_to BETWEEN CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AND CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL '7 days';
-- 命中：idx_m11_consent_expiring（部分索引）
-- 期望：P95 < 15ms
```

**C.9 停台原因分布（运营分析）**

```sql
SELECT reason_code, reason_category, is_hospital_side,
       COUNT(*) AS cnt,
       ROUND(COUNT(*) * 100.0
             / SUM(COUNT(*)) OVER (), 2) AS ratio_pct
FROM m11_reservation_change
WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
  AND change_type IN ('CANCEL', 'POSTPONED')
  AND original_surgery_date BETWEEN '2026-01-01' AND '2026-10-31'
GROUP BY reason_code, reason_category, is_hospital_side
ORDER BY cnt DESC;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_chg_date
-- 期望：P95 < 50ms
```

**C.10 手术间效率与安全指标（P08 + 质控）**

```sql
SELECT summary_date, room_code, planned_count, completed_count,
       completion_rate, on_time_rate, avg_turnover_minutes,
       max_delay_minutes, checklist_blocked_count, eye_mismatch_count
FROM m11_reservation_daily
WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
  AND dim_level = 'ROOM'
  AND summary_date BETWEEN CURRENT_DATE - 30 AND CURRENT_DATE
ORDER BY summary_date DESC, room_code;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_daily_date
-- 期望：P95 < 10ms（预聚合，O(1) 查询）
```

**C.11 安全相关审计核查（合规）**

```sql
SELECT log_no, reservation_no, patient_name_mask, action,
       change_summary, change_reason, approval_no,
       operator_name, operator_role, operated_at
FROM m11_reservation_log
WHERE is_safety_related = TRUE
  AND operated_at >= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '90 days'
ORDER BY operated_at DESC;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_log_safety（部分索引）
-- 期望：P95 < 60ms（只扫近 3 个分区）
```

**C.12 流程瓶颈分析（状态停留时长）**

```sql
SELECT to_status,
       COUNT(*) AS times,
       ROUND(AVG(dwell_seconds) / 3600.0, 2) AS avg_hours,
       MAX(dwell_seconds) / 3600 AS max_hours,
       COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE dwell_level = 'STUCK') AS stuck_times
FROM m11_reservation_status_log
WHERE operated_at >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '90 days'
  AND dwell_seconds IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY to_status
ORDER BY avg_hours DESC;
-- 命中：idx_m11_resv_stlog_status
-- 期望：P95 < 120ms
```

## 附录 D：配置参数与字典

**D.1 系统参数**

| 参数编码 | 名称 | 类型 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `M11.RESV.CODE_CACHE_SIZE` | 预约号流水缓存块 | int | 200 | Redis 预分配块大小 |
| `M11.RESV.MAX_ADVANCE_DAYS` | 最大提前预约天数 | int | 90 | 超过须科主任审批 |
| `M11.RESV.MIN_ADVANCE_HOURS` | 最小提前预约小时 | int | 12 | 术前检查结果有效期约束 |
| `M11.RESV.MONTHLY_LIMIT_PER_SURGEON` | 单医师月手术量上限 | int | 120 | 超出提示排班过载 |
| `M11.RESV.DAILY_LIMIT_PER_ROOM` | 单手术间日台次上限 | int | 18 | 排台超限预警 |
| `M11.RESV.CANCEL_APPROVAL_HOURS` | 停台需审批的小时门槛 | int | 24 | 手术前 24 小时内停台必须审批 |
| `M11.RESV.PENALTY_HOURS` | 扣违约金的门槛小时 | int | 24 | 患方 24 小时内取消扣违约金 |
| `M11.RESV.CHANGE_MAX_TIMES` | 单预约最大改期次数 | int | 3 | 超限须重新登记 |
| `M11.RESV.CONSENT_VALID_HOURS` | 同意书有效期小时 | int | 72 | 超期须重签 |
| `M11.RESV.CONSENT_EXPIRE_WARN_HOURS` | 同意书过期预警小时 | int | 48 | 提前提醒 |
| `M11.RESV.CONSENT_REQUIRED_SIGNERS` | 默认签署方数量 | int | 2 | 手术 + 麻醉各 2 方 |
| `M11.RESV.CONSENT_IMPLANT_SIGNERS` | 植入类签署方数量 | int | 3 | ICL/PRL 需第三方见证 |
| `M11.RESV.MED_INTERVAL_TOLERANCE_MIN` | 点药间隔容差分钟 | int | 2 | 间隔判定允许的误差 |
| `M11.RESV.MED_MISS_WARN_MIN` | 漏点药预警分钟 | int | 15 | 超过计划时间多久告警 |
| `M11.RESV.CHECKLIST_REQUIRED_ROUNDS` | 必需核对轮次 | string | WARD,OR_ENTRY,TIME_OUT | 缺任一轮不可开台 |
| `M11.RESV.TIMEOUT_MIN_PARTICIPANTS` | Time-Out 最少参与人数 | int | 3 | 安全核查制度要求 |
| `M11.RESV.EYE_MISMATCH_BLOCK` | 眼别不一致强制阻断 | bool | true | **禁止关闭**，关闭需模块负责人审批 |
| `M11.RESV.EYE_CHANGE_NEED_APPROVAL` | 眼别变更需审批 | bool | true | 禁止关闭 |
| `M11.RESV.STUCK_DWELL_HOURS` | 滞留判定小时 | int | 48 | 超过即标 STUCK |
| `M11.RESV.SLOW_DWELL_HOURS` | 偏慢判定小时 | int | 24 | 超过即标 SLOW |
| `M11.RESV.M06_PUSH_RETRY_TIMES` | M06 推送重试次数 | int | 3 | 失败后转人工 |
| `M11.RESV.M06_PUSH_TIMEOUT_MS` | M06 推送超时毫秒 | int | 3000 | — |
| `M11.RESV.P09_IDEM_TTL_HOURS` | P-09 幂等键有效期小时 | int | 24 | — |
| `M11.RESV.FOLLOWUP_AUTO_GENERATE` | 术后自动生成随访 | bool | true | P-09 触发 M11-06 |
| `M11.RESV.LOG_RETENTION_MONTHS` | 审计日志保留月数 | int | 36 | 合规 3 年 |
| `M11.RESV.STLOG_RETENTION_MONTHS` | 状态日志保留月数 | int | 36 | — |
| `M11.RESV.BOARD_REFRESH_SECONDS` | 看板自动刷新秒 | int | 30 | 前端轮询间隔 |
| `M11.RESV.BOARD_CACHE_SECONDS` | 看板缓存秒 | int | 10 | — |

**D.2 字典项**

| 字典编码 | 名称 | 取值 |
|---|---|---|
| `M11_REFRACTIVE_TYPE` | 屈光术式 | `SMILE` / `FS_LASIK` / `TRANSPRK` / `SMART` / `ICL` / `PRL` / `CXL` / `PIOL` / `RK` / `OTHER_REFRACTIVE` |
| `M11_EYE_SIDE` | 手术眼别 | `OD` / `OS` / `OU`（本子模块禁 `NA`） |
| `M11_ANEST_TYPE` | 麻醉方式 | `SURFACE` / `LOCAL` / `SEDATION` / `GENERAL` |
| `M11_CANCEL_REASON` | 取消原因 | `PATIENT_REQUEST` / `MEDICAL` / `SCHEDULE` / `EQUIPMENT` / `WEATHER` / `FINANCE` / `OTHER` |
| `M11_CHANGE_REASON` | 变更原因 | 见附录 B |
| `M11_CONSENT_TYPE` | 同意书类型 | `SURGERY` / `ANESTHESIA` / `IMPLANT` / `IOL` / `OTHER` |
| `M11_DRUG_CATEGORY` | 用药类别 | 见附录 B |
| `M11_EDU_TYPE` | 宣教类型 | 见附录 B |
| `M11_CHECK_ROUND` | 核对轮次 | `WARD` / `OR_ENTRY` / `TIME_OUT` / `PRE_DISCHARGE` |
| `M11_ROOM_CODE` | 手术间 | `OR-1` / `OR-2` / `OR-3`（由 M01 手术间主数据维护） |
| `M11_NOTIFY_CHANNEL` | 通知渠道 | `SMS` / `WECHAT` / `PHONE` / `APP` |
