## 附录 E：建表依赖顺序与 Alembic 迁移

**E.1 外部依赖（必须先于本子模块存在）**

| 顺序 | 表/对象 | 所属模块 | 本子模块的使用方式 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `mpi_patient` | M03-01 | `m11_reservation.patient_id` 只读引用 |
| 2 | `mdm_surgery_operation` | M01-07 | 术式与手术操作编码配置（只读） |
| 3 | `m11_surgery_plan` | M11-04 | `plan_no` 只读引用（P-04） |
| 4 | `m11_preop_exam` | M11-02 | `exam_no` 只读引用（P-10 准入判定） |
| 5 | `m11_refractive_record` | M11-03 | `record_no` 只读引用（P-02） |
| 6 | `m06_day_apply` | M06-01 | `m06_apply_id` 只读引用 |
| 7 | PostgreSQL 扩展 `pg_trgm` | PG 16 | 患者姓名模糊检索 GIN 索引 |

> **注意：** 本子模块**不建物理外键**，因此外部表存在与否不影响 DDL 执行；上表列出的是**数据可用性**前置条件（运行期调用上游接口时数据须存在）。

**E.2 本子模块建表顺序**

```
                   ┌──────────────────────────────┐
                   │ ① m11_reservation            │  预约主表，无内部依赖
                   │   （核心，先建）                │
                   └──────────────┬───────────────┘
                                  │
    ┌──────────────┬──────────────┼──────────────┬──────────────┐
    │ ②            │ ③            │ ④            │ ⑤            │ ⑥
    ▼              ▼              ▼              ▼              ▼
┌──────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐
│medication│ │education   │ │consent     │ │checklist   │ │change      │
│术前用药   │ │术前宣教     │ │知情同意书    │ │术前核对     │ │停台改期     │
└──────────┘ └────────────┘ └────────────┘ └────────────┘ └────────────┘
                                  │
    ┌─────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┐
    │ ⑦                                                        │ ⑧
    ▼                                                          ▼
┌────────────────────────┐                        ┌────────────────────────┐
│status_log（分区表）      │                        │reservation_daily       │
│预约状态流转日志          │                        │手术日看板日汇总          │
└────────────────────────┘                        └────────────────────────┘
                                  │
                                  │ ⑨
                                  ▼
                     ┌────────────────────────┐
                     │reservation_log（分区表） │
                     │操作审计日志              │
                     └────────────────────────┘
```

**E.3 Alembic 迁移脚本**

```python
"""M11-05 手术预约与术前准备 —— 建表迁移

revision = "20261101_m11_05"
down_revision = "20261101_m11_04"
"""

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision = "20261101_m11_05"
down_revision = "20261101_m11_04"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None


def upgrade() -> None:
    """按依赖顺序建表：主表 → 四张准备明细 → 停台改期 → 两张分区日志 → 日汇总。"""
    op.execute("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm")

    # 1. 预约主表
    op.create_table(
        "m11_reservation",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("reservation_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("empi_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("patient_name", sa.String(64), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_gender", sa.String(8), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("patient_age", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("patient_phone", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("id_card_mask", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("record_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("plan_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("plan_version", sa.Integer(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("exam_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("admission_result_snap", sa.String(16), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("optometry_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("refractive_type", sa.String(24), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("operation_code", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("operation_name", sa.String(200), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("eye_side", sa.String(8), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("eye_side_plan_snap", sa.String(8), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("laterality_mark", sa.String(8), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("laterality_marked_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("laterality_marked_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("laterality_witness_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("surgery_date", sa.Date(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("surgery_weekday", sa.String(8), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("time_slot", sa.String(16), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("planned_start_time", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("planned_end_time", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("sequence_no", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=True),

                  server_default=sa.text("1")),

                  server_default="DRAFT"),

        sa.Column("consent_completed", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,

                  server_default=sa.text("0")),

        sa.Column("actual_end_time", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("surgery_completed_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("surgery_outcome_snap", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("intraop_summary_snap", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("followup_plan_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("followup_generated", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("cancel_reason", sa.String(512), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("cancel_reason_code", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("cancelled_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("cancelled_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("postpone_reason", sa.String(512), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("no_show_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),

        sa.Column("ready_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("remark", sa.String(1024), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("ext_json", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("version", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("0")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("created_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),

    op.create_index("uk_m11_reservation_no", "m11_reservation",
                    ["reservation_no"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_index("uk_m11_reservation_patient_date_eye", "m11_reservation",
                    ["patient_id", "surgery_date", "eye_side"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "is_deleted = FALSE AND status NOT IN "
                        "('CANCELLED','POSTPONED','NO_SHOW')"))
    op.create_index("idx_m11_reservation_date", "m11_reservation",
                    ["surgery_date", "room_code", "sequence_no"])
    op.create_index("idx_m11_reservation_patient", "m11_reservation",
                    ["patient_id", "surgery_date"])
    op.create_index("idx_m11_reservation_active", "m11_reservation",
                    ["surgery_date", "status"],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "is_deleted = FALSE AND status IN "
                        "('SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED','PREPARING','READY','IN_SURGERY')"))
    op.create_index("idx_m11_reservation_name_trgm", "m11_reservation",
                    ["patient_name"], postgresql_using="gin",
                    postgresql_ops={"patient_name": "gin_trgm_ops"})

    # 2~6. 术前准备明细表（用药/宣教/同意书/核对/停台改期）
    for tbl, extra_cols in _detail_tables().items():
        op.create_table(tbl, *extra_cols)

    # 7. 状态流转日志（按月分区）
    op.create_table(
        "m11_reservation_status_log",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("reservation_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("reservation_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("from_status", sa.String(16), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("to_status", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("transition_action", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("transition_source", sa.String(24), nullable=False,
                  server_default="USER"),
        sa.Column("dwell_seconds", sa.Integer(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("dwell_level", sa.String(16), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("status_reason", sa.String(512), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("change_snapshot", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("eye_side_snapshot", sa.String(8), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("related_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("operator_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("operator_name", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("operator_role", sa.String(16), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("operated_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("trace_id", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("org_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("1")),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.PrimaryKeyConstraint("id", "operated_at",
                                name="pk_m11_reservation_status_log"),
        postgresql_partition_by="RANGE (operated_at)",
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_status_log_p202611 "
        "PARTITION OF m11_reservation_status_log "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01')")
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_status_log_pdefault "
        "PARTITION OF m11_reservation_status_log DEFAULT")

    # 8. 手术日看板日汇总
    op.create_table(
        "m11_reservation_daily",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("summary_date", sa.Date(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("surgeon_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("surgeon_name", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("room_code", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("facility_name", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("dim_level", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="DETAIL"),
        sa.Column("planned_count", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("0")),
        sa.Column("completed_count", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("0")),
        sa.Column("completion_rate", sa.Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("eye_mismatch_count", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("0")),
        sa.Column("refresh_mode", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="HOURLY"),
        sa.Column("last_refreshed_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("org_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("1")),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
    )

    # 9. 操作审计日志（按月分区）
    op.create_table(
        "m11_reservation_log",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("log_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("reservation_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("reservation_no", sa.String(32), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("patient_name_mask", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("action", sa.String(48), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("action_group", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="BUSINESS"),
        sa.Column("is_sensitive", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("is_safety_related", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("result", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="SUCCESS"),
        sa.Column("operated_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.PrimaryKeyConstraint("id", "operated_at",
                                name="pk_m11_reservation_log"),
        postgresql_partition_by="RANGE (operated_at)",
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_log_p202611 "
        "PARTITION OF m11_reservation_log "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01')")
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m11_reservation_log_pdefault "
        "PARTITION OF m11_reservation_log DEFAULT")

    # 10. updated_at 触发器
    for tbl in ("m11_reservation", "m11_reservation_medication",
                "m11_reservation_education", "m11_consent",
                "m11_reservation_checklist", "m11_reservation_change",
                "m11_reservation_daily"):
        op.execute(
            "CREATE TRIGGER trg_%s_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON %s "
            "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_set_updated_at()" % (tbl, tbl))


def _detail_tables() -> dict:
    """返回明细表定义（供 upgrade 循环建表）。"""
    meta = sa.MetaData()
    tables = {}
    # 实际迁移中此处为完整的 sa.Column 列表，本附录只展示结构组织方式
    tables["m11_reservation_medication"] = []
    tables["m11_reservation_education"] = []
    tables["m11_consent"] = []
    tables["m11_reservation_checklist"] = []
    tables["m11_reservation_change"] = []
    return tables


def downgrade() -> None:
    """按反向依赖顺序回滚。"""
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_log")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_daily")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_status_log")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_change")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_checklist")
    op.drop_table("m11_consent")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_education")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation_medication")
    op.drop_table("m11_reservation")
```

**E.4 建表后校验清单**

| # | 校验项 | 期望结果 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `SELECT count(*) FROM pg_indexes WHERE tablename LIKE 'm11_reservation%' OR tablename LIKE 'm11_consent'` | ≥ 50 个索引 |
| 2 | `SELECT count(*) FROM pg_constraint WHERE conname LIKE 'ck_m11_resv%' OR conname LIKE 'ck_m11_consent%'` | ≥ 120 个 CHECK 约束 |
| 3 | `SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class WHERE relname LIKE 'm11_reservation_log_p%'` | 分区数 = 预建月数 + 1（DEFAULT） |
| 4 | 插入 `eye_side = 'NA'` 的预约 | 违反 `ck_m11_resv_eye_side` |
| 5 | 插入 `eye_side != eye_side_plan_snap` 的预约 | 违反 `ck_m11_resv_eye_matches_plan` |
| 6 | 插入 `check_result = 'PASSED'` 且 `eye_side_match = FALSE` 的核对 | 违反 `ck_m11_resv_chk_passed_eye` |
| 7 | 插入 `eye_side_match` 与服务层计算不一致的核对 | 违反 `ck_m11_resv_chk_eye_consistent` |
| 8 | 插入 `sign_status = 'SIGNED'` 但 `content_hash` 非 64 位十六进制 | 违反 `ck_m11_consent_hash_format` |
| 9 | 直接 `DELETE FROM m11_consent` | 生产环境由权限禁止，仅允许 `is_deleted` |
| 10 | `UPDATE m11_reservation_log SET action = 'X'` | 生产环境由权限禁止（只追加） |

---

## 附录 F：文档维护约定

| 约定 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| 变更入口 | 表结构以本文档为准；变更后须同步更新《详细设计》第 4 章指引块表数 |
| 命名不可变 | 已发布的表名、字段名不可改名（影响 M06/M09/M16-04 的只读引用） |
| 新增字段 | 只允许在表尾部追加可空字段，禁止在中间插入（避免代码按位置映射） |
| 金额字段 | 新增金额相关字段必须以 `_snap` 结尾，且注释写明「由 M16 提供，M11 不计算」 |
| 眼别字段 | 任何新增眼别字段必须为 `VARCHAR(8)` + `CHECK IN ('OD','OS','OU')`，禁用 `NA` |
| 枚举扩展 | 术式枚举必须与 M01-07 3.1.4 同步，禁止本模块自创术式码 |
| 分区治理 | 两张日志表每月 1 日 01:00 预建未来 3 个月分区，超 36 个月归档后 `DETACH` |
| 不可删除表 | `m11_consent`、`m11_reservation_checklist`、`m11_reservation_change` 永不物理删除 |
| 敏感字段 | 新增敏感字段须登记到 `m11_reservation_log` 的 `is_sensitive` 留痕范围 |
| 安全字段 | 新增与眼别/核对相关的字段必须纳入 `is_safety_related` 审计范围 |
